# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 035 GREAT DEPRESSION 2.0 EFFECTS
# ============================================================================
# This file owns the Event 35 transaction gateway, national Severity lifecycle,
# Depression Center registry, recovery doctrines, contagion and Social Collapse
# adapters, the Evolution III global episode, and compact permanent receipts.
# The public gateway is `great_depression_start_or_deepen`. Every external
# caller supplies a complete contract. Invalid or duplicate calls return a
# result without changing the active crisis.
# ============================================================================

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Runtime and bounded registry helpers
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

great_depression_initialize_global_runtime = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_runtime_initialized } }
		clear_array = global.great_depression_active_countries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_active_country_episode_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_sources
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_targets
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_stage_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_receipt_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_episode_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_stage_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_values
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_receipts
		clear_array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_episode_entries
		clear_array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry_entries
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_next_episode_id = constant:great_depression_api.minimum_episode_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_next_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.minimum_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_episode_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.none }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_severe_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_new_major_crisis_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_contribution_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.none }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.none }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_relief_total = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_global_flag = great_depression_runtime_initialized
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_stage_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_conversion_count } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_activation_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.none }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_highest_stage } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.none }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_announcement_receipt_id } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_announcement_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_receipt_id } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_day } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Event 35 records only the portion of its own Chaos change
# that the shared meter can actually apply. This keeps outcome receipts
# idempotent and gives recovery a bounded ledger from which it can repay relief.
great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = settings_chaos_meter_disabled }
			check_variable = { var = chaos_change value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_applied_chaos_change = chaos_change }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.chaos_meter_value }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_chaos_room = constant:chaos_meter_tier_range.tier_final.min }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_chaos_room = global.chaos_meter_value }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_chaos_room value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_chaos_room = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_applied_chaos_change value = great_depression_chaos_room compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_applied_chaos_change = great_depression_chaos_room }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_applied_chaos_change value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = great_depression_applied_chaos_change }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason = constant:chaos_meter_history_reason.generic.increase }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason_custom = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_applied_chaos_change }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_applied_chaos_change }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Global Evolution III receipts use the same meter cap but are
# kept on the worldwide ledger, because no converted recipient owns the
# worldwide activation consequence.
great_depression_apply_global_chaos_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = settings_chaos_meter_disabled }
			check_variable = { var = chaos_change value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change = chaos_change }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.chaos_meter_value }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_chaos_room = constant:chaos_meter_tier_range.tier_final.min }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_chaos_room = global.chaos_meter_value }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_chaos_room value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_chaos_room = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change value = great_depression_global_chaos_room compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change = great_depression_global_chaos_room }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason = constant:chaos_meter_history_reason.generic.increase }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason_custom = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total }
				add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_applied_global_chaos_change }
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Positive recovery can repay only Event 35's own applied
# increase and never more than the live meter contains.
great_depression_apply_strong_recovery_chaos_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_outcome value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.strong compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_strong_recovery_chaos_receipt }
			has_variable = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = settings_chaos_meter_disabled }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief = constant:great_depression_event.strong_recovery_chaos_relief }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total value = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = global.chaos_meter_value
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief = global.chaos_meter_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief }
			multiply_temp_variable = { chaos_change = -1 }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason = constant:chaos_meter_history_reason.generic.decrease }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason_custom = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief }
			set_variable = { great_depression_event35_chaos_relief_total = great_depression_recovery_chaos_relief }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_strong_recovery_chaos_receipt
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Worldwide recovery repays only the global Event 35 receipt.
great_depression_apply_international_recovery_chaos_relief = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_international_recovery_chaos_receipt }
			has_variable = global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = settings_chaos_meter_disabled }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief = constant:great_depression_event.international_recovery_chaos_relief }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total value = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief compare = less_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief = global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = global.chaos_meter_value
				check_variable = { var = global.chaos_meter_value value = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief compare = less_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief = global.chaos_meter_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief }
			multiply_temp_variable = { chaos_change = -1 }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason = constant:chaos_meter_history_reason.generic.decrease }
			set_temp_variable = { chaos_history_reason_custom = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			add_chaos_meter_value = yes
			subtract_from_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_relief_total = great_depression_global_recovery_chaos_relief }
			set_global_flag = great_depression_international_recovery_chaos_receipt
		}
	}
}

great_depression_initialize_country_runtime = {
	great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_depression_severity } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_doctrine } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_doctrine = constant:great_depression_doctrine.unassigned }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_phase } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_center_count } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_evolution_floor } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.baseline }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_ai_profile } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_ai_profile = constant:great_depression_ai_profile.relief_coalition }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_social_extreme_days } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_committed_receipt_ids^num } }
		clear_array = great_depression_committed_receipt_ids
	}
	great_depression_incident_initialize_history = yes
}

great_depression_allocate_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_next_receipt_id } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_next_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.minimum_receipt_id }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_receipt_id = global.great_depression_next_receipt_id }
	add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_next_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.receipt_increment }
}

# COUNTRY scope. Build the complete independent Event 35 contract for the
# automatic event pool or the Negative Economy cluster. Cluster dispatch has
# already selected this country, so it uses the consequence pacing lane and
# never increments the normal independent-event pacing counter.
great_depression_prepare_independent_call = {
	great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_contract_supplied = constant:great_depression_api.contract_version }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_caller_type = constant:great_depression_caller.independent_event_35 }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_event_id = constant:great_depression_api.event_35_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_episode_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_pacing_mode = constant:great_depression_pacing.normal_event }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = event_cluster_member_fire_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_pacing_mode = constant:great_depression_pacing.consequence_no_pacing }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_history_mode = constant:great_depression_history_mode.normal }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_report_policy = constant:great_depression_report_policy.queue }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_news_policy = constant:great_depression_news_policy.queue }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.baseline }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_input_mode = constant:great_depression_severity_input.severity_profile }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.independent }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.independent_severe }
}

great_depression_allocate_episode = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_next_episode_id } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_next_episode_id = constant:great_depression_api.minimum_episode_id }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_episode_id = global.great_depression_next_episode_id }
	add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_next_episode_id = constant:great_depression_api.episode_increment }
}

great_depression_register_active_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_countries value = THIS } }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.great_depression_active_countries^num
					value = constant:great_depression_event.active_country_registry_cap
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_country_episode_entries value = great_depression_active_episode_id }
	}
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_active_country_count = global.great_depression_active_countries^num }
}

great_depression_unregister_active_country = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_countries value = THIS } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_registry_remove_index = 0 }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_active_countries^num
			value = great_depression_registry_scan_index
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_active_countries^great_depression_registry_scan_index value = THIS compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_registry_remove_index = great_depression_registry_scan_index }
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_countries index = great_depression_registry_remove_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_active_country_episode_entries index = great_depression_registry_remove_index }
	}
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_active_country_count = global.great_depression_active_countries^num }
}

great_depression_refresh_costs = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_factor = constant:great_depression_scaling.medium_cost_factor }
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects < constant:great_depression_scaling.small_economy_civilian_limit }
		set_variable = { great_depression_cost_factor = constant:great_depression_scaling.small_cost_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:great_depression_scaling.large_economy_civilian_limit }
		set_variable = { great_depression_cost_factor = constant:great_depression_scaling.large_cost_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_cost_factor
				value = constant:great_depression_scaling.minimum_cost_factor
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_cost_factor = constant:great_depression_scaling.minimum_cost_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_cost_factor
				value = constant:great_depression_scaling.maximum_cost_factor
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_cost_factor = constant:great_depression_scaling.maximum_cost_factor }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_civilian = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_civilian }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_civilian = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_civilian
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_political_power = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_political_power }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_political_power = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_political_power
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_manpower = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_manpower }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_manpower = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_manpower
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_fuel = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_fuel }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_fuel = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_fuel
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_trains = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_trains }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_trains = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_trains
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_convoys = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_convoys }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_convoys = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_convoys
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_support_equipment = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_support_equipment }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_support_equipment = great_depression_cost_factor }
	round_variable = great_depression_cost_support_equipment
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_stability = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_stability }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_stability = great_depression_cost_factor }
	set_variable = { great_depression_cost_war_support = constant:great_depression_cost.standard_war_support }
	multiply_variable = { great_depression_cost_war_support = great_depression_cost_factor }
}

great_depression_pay_action_cost = {
	# Each family deliberately spends at most four resource types. The
	# decision trigger is shared, while this branch is the authoritative debit.
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.doctrine_choice compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.policy_review compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.emergency_relief compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.foreign_support compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_restructure compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_abandon compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.finance_trade compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.austerity compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.state_planning compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.market_clear compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.contagion compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.social_response compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.global_reconstruction compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_political_power }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_political_power = great_depression_payment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.emergency_relief compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_protection compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_reopen compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.public_works compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.social_response compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_manpower }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_manpower = great_depression_payment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.emergency_relief compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_reopen compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.public_works compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.finance_trade compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.state_planning compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.contagion compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.global_reconstruction compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_fuel }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_fuel = great_depression_payment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_protection compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_reopen compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_restructure compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.public_works compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.state_planning compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.social_response compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_trains }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = great_depression_payment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.foreign_support compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.finance_trade compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.contagion compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.global_reconstruction compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_convoys }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = great_depression_payment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_protection compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_restructure compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_support_equipment }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = great_depression_payment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.policy_review compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.austerity compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_abandon compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.market_clear compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.social_response compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_stability }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_stability = great_depression_payment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.austerity compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_abandon compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.market_clear compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_war_support }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = -1 }
		add_war_support = great_depression_payment
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.emergency_relief compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.foreign_support compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_protection compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_reopen compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.center_restructure compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.finance_trade compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.state_planning compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.contagion compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_cost_family value = constant:great_depression_cost_family.global_reconstruction compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_payment = great_depression_cost_civilian }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment_major }
		if = {
			limit = { great_depression_cost_civilian > constant:great_depression_cost.civilian_commitment_major_threshold }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment_major }
		}
		else = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment }
		}
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Depression Center registry
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

great_depression_clear_center_state_flags = {
	great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
	great_depression_clear_center_treatment_flags = yes
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
	clear_variable = great_depression_center_episode_id
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_factory_occupation_active
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_factory_occupation_resolved
	clear_variable = great_depression_center_factory_occupation_episode_id
}

# Physical stage and policy treatment are separate state facts. Stage changes
# use this helper so a paid relief policy remains active while a center worsens.
great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = {
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_distressed }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_idled }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_abandoned }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_reopened }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_hollowed }
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_restructured
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_scarred
}

# State scope helper. The indexed center ledger is the durable authority for
# physical outcomes while state flags remain the fast consumer for decisions
# and modifiers. The caller sets great_depression_center_ledger_stage first.
great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_registry_index
			has_variable = great_depression_center_ledger_stage
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_ledger_index = great_depression_center_registry_index }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_ledger_stage_value = great_depression_center_ledger_stage }
		ROOT = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage_entries^great_depression_ledger_index = great_depression_ledger_stage_value }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_clear_center_treatment_flags = {
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_public_works }
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_finance_restored
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_trusteeship
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_asset_reorganization
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_strategic
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_essential
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_lasting_capacity
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_occupation_broken
	clear_variable = great_depression_center_protection_expiry_day
}

# The center project ledger is deliberately state-scoped. A project consumes
# its national inputs when authorized, then earns its resolution only after a
# bounded period of controlled, viable work. This keeps a reopened center from
# becoming an instant free factory or a recovery shortcut.

great_depression_set_center_project_stage = {
	# Input: ROOT.great_depression_next_center_project_stage.
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_next_center_project_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.authorized compare = equals } }
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_next_center_project_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.active_work compare = equals } }
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_next_center_project_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.paused compare = equals } }
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_next_center_project_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.certification compare = equals } }
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage = great_depression_next_center_project_stage }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage_day = global.num_days }
}

great_depression_authorize_center_project = {
	# Country scope supplies great_depression_pending_center_project; this
	# effect is called in the selected state scope after the cost is paid.
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
				has_variable = great_depression_pending_center_project
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active }
			}
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			is_owned_by = ROOT
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			check_variable = {
				var = ROOT.great_depression_pending_center_project
				value = constant:great_depression_center_project.none
				compare = greater_than
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = ROOT.great_depression_pending_center_project
				value = constant:great_depression_center_project.protection
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_type = ROOT.great_depression_pending_center_project }
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt
		ROOT = {
		set_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_started_day = global.num_days }
		clear_variable = great_depression_pending_center_project
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.authorized }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_start_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_last_process_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_due_day = global.num_days }
	add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_project_due_day = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_total_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_pause_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_pause_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_work_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_paralysis_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_next_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.authorized }
	great_depression_set_center_project_stage = yes
	}
}

great_depression_fail_center_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt }
		}
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.failed }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage_day = global.num_days }
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
			ROOT = {
				clr_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active
				clr_country_flag = great_depression_public_works_project_active
				set_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_failure_recorded
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_failure_count }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_project_failure_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
				else = { set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_failure_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_variable = great_depression_failed_response_count }
					add_to_variable = { great_depression_failed_response_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { great_depression_failed_response_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_action_delta = constant:great_depression_action.mission_failure }
			great_depression_apply_public_action_delta = yes
		}
	}
}

great_depression_complete_center_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed_receipt }
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_center_project_type
				value = constant:great_depression_center_project.none
				compare = greater_than
			}
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_center_project_type
				value = constant:great_depression_center_project.protection
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed_receipt
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_state = great_depression_center_project_type }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.reopened_capacity compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.transport_integration compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.public_works compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.lasting_capacity compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.international_reconstruction compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened } }
				ROOT = { great_depression_increment_reopened_center_count = yes }
			}
			great_depression_clear_center_state_flags = yes
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_restructured
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_reopened }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.public_works compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_public_works }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_completed_receipt } }
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_completed_receipt
					ROOT = {
						if = {
							limit = { has_variable = great_depression_public_works_project_count }
							add_to_variable = { great_depression_public_works_project_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
						}
						else = { set_variable = { great_depression_public_works_project_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_finance_restored
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_trusteeship
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_essential
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_asset_reorganization
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals } }
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_strategic
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_essential
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.lasting_capacity compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_lasting_capacity
				ROOT = {
					set_country_flag = great_depression_lasting_capacity_created
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = great_depression_lasting_capacity_count }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_lasting_capacity_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
					}
					else = { set_variable = { great_depression_lasting_capacity_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.international_reconstruction compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_public_works }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals }
					}
				}
				great_depression_record_center_positive_resolution = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.strategic_rescue compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.restructured }
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.restructured }
				great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.reopened }
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.reopened }
				great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
			}
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.completed }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_stage_day = global.num_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_type value = constant:great_depression_center_project.reopened_capacity compare = equals }
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_loss_receipt
				industrial_complex < constant:great_depression_api.one
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = constant:great_depression_api.one
				instant_build = no
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_failure_recorded
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_public_works_project_active
			set_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed
		}
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
	clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
	}
}

}

great_depression_process_center_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
			}
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
					ROOT = { NOT = { uses_normal_civilian_systems = yes } }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_pause_day = global.num_days }
				set_variable = { great_depression_next_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.paused }
				great_depression_set_center_project_stage = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_pause_days = global.num_days }
				subtract_from_variable = { great_depression_center_project_pause_days = great_depression_center_project_pause_day }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_pause_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_pause_fail_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					great_depression_fail_center_project = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_project_pause_elapsed = global.num_days }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_project_pause_elapsed = great_depression_center_project_pause_day }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_project_due_day = great_depression_project_pause_elapsed }
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_last_process_day = global.num_days }
				set_variable = { great_depression_next_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.active_work }
				great_depression_set_center_project_stage = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_project_work_elapsed = global.num_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_project_work_elapsed = great_depression_center_project_last_process_day }
			if = {
				limit = { great_depression_project_work_elapsed > constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_project_work_days = great_depression_project_work_elapsed }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_last_process_day = global.num_days }
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_project_paralysis_days = great_depression_project_work_elapsed }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { great_depression_center_project_paralysis_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_paralysis_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_pause_fail_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_work_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_total_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
				}
				if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_work_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_total_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } } great_depression_complete_center_project = yes }
				else = { great_depression_fail_center_project = yes }
			}
			else = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.authorized compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_work_days value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
					}
					set_variable = { great_depression_next_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.active_work }
					great_depression_set_center_project_stage = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_work_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_total_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					}
					great_depression_complete_center_project = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_project_work_days value = constant:great_depression_event.center_project_certification_start_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification }
					}
					set_variable = { great_depression_next_center_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.certification }
					great_depression_set_center_project_stage = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_process_center_projects = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_center_project_scan_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_center_project_scan_index = {
				great_depression_process_center_project = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_reconcile_center_transfer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			OR = {
				NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_receipt }
		}
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_receipt
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_contract_open
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_transfer_episode_id = ROOT.great_depression_active_episode_id }
		ROOT = {
			# The old owner stops paying immediately. The state keeps its project
			# progress and completed receipts for the successor contract.
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_public_works_project_active
			set_country_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_disqualifier
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_center_selected
			clear_variable = great_depression_selected_center_state
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_transfer_count }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_transfer_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_transfer_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
		}
		great_depression_submit_humanitarian_center_pressure = yes
	}
}

great_depression_process_center_transfers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_center_transfer_scan_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_center_transfer_scan_index = {
				great_depression_reconcile_center_transfer = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_adopt_transferred_center = {
	# Called in a state scope by the successor country after selecting a centre
	# with a transfer receipt. State-scoped project progress and resolution
	# receipts remain authoritative; the country registry only gains one aligned
	# row and resumes the burden once the successor explicitly adopts it.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_receipt
			is_owned_by = ROOT
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			ROOT = {
				great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_count value = constant:great_depression_event.center_registry_cap compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = great_depression_transfer_adoption_state
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.reopened }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.liquidated }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.abandoned }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project = constant:great_depression_center_project.none }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_state }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project = great_depression_center_project_state }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_type }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project = great_depression_center_project_type }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_loss_receipt = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_loss_receipt }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_loss_receipt = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_civilian_baseline = building_level@industrial_complex }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_military_baseline = building_level@arms_factory }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.none }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_start_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_due_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_pause_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_stage }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage = great_depression_center_project_stage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_start_day }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_start_day = great_depression_center_project_start_day }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_stage_day }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage_day = great_depression_center_project_stage_day }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_due_day }
			set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_due_day = great_depression_center_project_due_day }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_project_pause_day }
		set_variable = { great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_pause_day = great_depression_center_project_pause_day }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_registry_index = ROOT.great_depression_center_count }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage }
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = great_depression_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state } } }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_stage_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_loss_receipt }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_civilian_baseline }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_military_baseline }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_start_day_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_start_day }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_day_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage_day }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_due_day_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_due_day }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_pause_day_entries value = event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state.great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_pause_day }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_owner_country_entries value = ROOT }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_adopted
			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:great_depression_transfer_adoption_state = {
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active
			}
		}
		great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_adopted_receipt
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_contract_open
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_stage
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_loss_receipt
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_civilian_baseline
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_military_baseline
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_start_day
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_stage_day
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_due_day
		clear_variable = great_depression_transfer_adopted_project_pause_day
	}
}

great_depression_set_center_status = {
	# Input: great_depression_center_status, great_depression_center_state_id
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = great_depression_center_status_target }
		event_target:great_depression_center_status_target = {
			great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_distressed }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_idled }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.abandoned compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_abandoned }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.liquidated compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_hollowed }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.reopened compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_reopened }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.restructured compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_restructured
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_center_status value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.scarred compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_scarred
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = ROOT.great_depression_center_status }
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = ROOT.great_depression_center_status }
			great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
		}
	}
}

great_depression_apply_center_treatment = {
	# Input: great_depression_treatment, selected state event target.
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = ROOT.great_depression_selected_center_state }
		var:ROOT.great_depression_selected_center_state = {
			great_depression_clear_center_treatment_flags = yes
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_treatment value = constant:great_depression_center_project.public_works compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_public_works }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_treatment value = constant:great_depression_center_project.finance_restoration compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_finance_restored
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_treatment value = constant:great_depression_center_project.trusteeship compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_trusteeship
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = ROOT.great_depression_treatment value = constant:great_depression_center_project.asset_reorganization compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_asset_reorganization
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_hollowed }
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_score_center_candidate = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = building_level@industrial_complex }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_industry_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_military = building_level@arms_factory }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_military = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_industry_weight }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = great_depression_center_candidate_military }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_naval = building_level@dockyard }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_naval = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_industry_weight }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = great_depression_center_candidate_naval }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_infrastructure = building_level@infrastructure }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_infrastructure = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_infrastructure_weight }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = great_depression_center_candidate_infrastructure }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_population = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_population = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_population_weight }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = great_depression_center_candidate_population }
	if = {
		limit = { is_capital = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_capital_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_neighbor_state = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center
			}
		}
