		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_score = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_adjacency_penalty }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Build a bounded weighted pool for the normal Event 35 event
# lane. The scheduler owns pacing, while this selector only chooses one valid
# country from the current usable-economy pool. Cluster dispatch never calls it.
great_depression_score_independent_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.base }
	if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.major }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = no }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.player }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_civilian_weight = num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_civilian_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.usable_civilian_factory }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_target_civilian_weight
		min = constant:great_depression_api.zero
		max = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.usable_civilian_factory_cap
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = great_depression_target_civilian_weight }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_military_weight = num_of_military_factories }
	multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_military_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.usable_military_factory }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_target_military_weight
		min = constant:great_depression_api.zero
		max = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.usable_military_factory_cap
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = great_depression_target_military_weight }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = oil amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = aluminium amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = rubber amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = tungsten amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = steel amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
				has_resources_in_country = { resource = chromium amount > constant:great_depression_target_signal.imported_resource_floor only_imported = yes }
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.high_trade_dependence }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_stability < constant:great_depression_target_signal.low_stability_ceiling }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.low_stability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_recent_industrial_expansion }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.recent_industrial_expansion }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				convoy_threat > constant:great_depression_target_signal.convoy_threat_floor
				has_convoys_war_support > constant:great_depression_target_signal.convoy_loss_malus_floor
				AND = {
					has_war = yes
					fuel_ratio < constant:great_depression_target_signal.critical_fuel_ratio
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.severe_supply_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_war_start_day
			has_war = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_war_days = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_war_days = great_depression_war_start_day }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_target_war_days value = constant:great_depression_target_signal.war_exhaustion_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				has_war_support < constant:great_depression_target_signal.war_exhaustion_support_ceiling
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.war_exhaustion }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_last_completed_event34_landing_state
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_last_completed_event34_landing_state value = constant:great_depression_event34_snapshot_state.failed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.rough_event34_landing }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_ever_started }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.previous_episode }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.recent_episode }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_recent_major_industrial_state_loss
			has_variable = great_depression_recent_major_industrial_state_loss_factories
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_industrial_loss_weight = great_depression_recent_major_industrial_state_loss_factories }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_target_industrial_loss_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.recent_major_industrial_state_loss_per_factory }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = great_depression_target_industrial_loss_weight
			min = constant:great_depression_api.zero
			max = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.recent_major_industrial_state_loss_cap
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = great_depression_target_industrial_loss_weight }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_refire_safeguard_active }
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_last_completed_episode_recovery_outcome
				value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.strong
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.strong_reform }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_refire_safeguard_active }
			OR = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_last_completed_episode_recovery_outcome
					value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.hollow
					compare = equals
				}
				has_idea = great_depression_scar_hollow_recovery
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_independent_target_weight = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.hollow_recovery }
	}
	round_temp_variable = great_depression_independent_target_weight
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_independent_target_weight
		min = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.minimum
		max = constant:great_depression_independent_target_weight.maximum
	}
}

# COUNTRY/root scope. Select one valid target using the weighted temporary
# array documented by the vanilla effects API, then execute the same complete
# contract used by cluster dispatch. The temporary array is always cleared.
great_depression_select_independent_target = {
	clear_temp_array = great_depression_independent_target_pool
	every_country = {
		limit = { great_depression_is_valid_independent_target = yes }
		great_depression_score_independent_target = yes
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_independent_target_weight
			value = great_depression_independent_target_pool_index
			add_to_temp_array = { array = great_depression_independent_target_pool }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_independent_target_pool^num
				value = constant:great_depression_api.zero
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = great_depression_independent_target_pool
			great_depression_prepare_independent_call = yes
			great_depression_start_or_deepen = yes
		}
	}
	clear_temp_array = great_depression_independent_target_pool
}

great_depression_register_initial_centers = {
	clear_array = great_depression_center_state_ids
	clear_array = great_depression_center_stage_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_stage_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_start_day_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_stage_day_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_due_day_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_project_pause_day_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_owner_country_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries
	set_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_required_center_count = constant:great_depression_event.minimum_center_cap }
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:great_depression_scaling.second_center_usable_civilian }
		set_variable = { great_depression_required_center_count = constant:great_depression_scaling.second_center_count }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:great_depression_scaling.third_center_usable_civilian }
		set_variable = { great_depression_required_center_count = constant:great_depression_event.normal_center_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > constant:great_depression_scaling.fourth_center_usable_civilian
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_required_center_count = constant:great_depression_event.center_registry_cap }
	}
	# Event 34 supplies one frozen Industrial Region target. Seed that state
	# before the score pass so inherited collapses preserve their reversal
	# instead of rerolling the opening registry from scratch.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = industrial_boom_inherited_region
			event_target:industrial_boom_inherited_region = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_initial_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_project_units value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_initial_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_initial_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
		}
		event_target:industrial_boom_inherited_region = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_registry_index = ROOT.great_depression_center_count }
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_state_ids value = event_target:industrial_boom_inherited_region }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_stage_entries value = great_depression_initial_center_stage }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project.none }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries value = building_level@industrial_complex }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries value = building_level@arms_factory }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.none }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_start_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_due_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_pause_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_owner_country_entries value = ROOT }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = great_depression_initial_center_stage }
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_episode_id = ROOT.great_depression_active_episode_id }
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			set_state_flag = great_depression_inherited_center
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_initial_center_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_initial_center_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_idled }
			}
			else = {
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_distressed }
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_selection_slots = great_depression_required_center_count }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_selection_slots = great_depression_center_count }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_center_selection_slots
		min = constant:great_depression_api.zero
		max = constant:great_depression_event.center_registry_cap
	}
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = great_depression_center_selection_slots
		value = great_depression_center_selection_index
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_saved = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_best_score = constant:great_depression_scaling.invalid_center_candidate_score }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_best_state_id = constant:great_depression_scaling.center_tie_break_sentinel }
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				impassable = no
				state_population_k > constant:great_depression_api.minimum_viable_state_population_k
				OR = {
					industrial_complex > constant:great_depression_api.zero
					arms_factory > constant:great_depression_api.zero
					dockyard > constant:great_depression_api.zero
				}
				NOT = { is_in_array = { array = ROOT.great_depression_center_state_ids value = THIS } }
			}
			great_depression_score_center_candidate = yes
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_state_id = id }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_candidate_score value = great_depression_center_best_score compare = greater_than }
						AND = {
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_candidate_score value = great_depression_center_best_score compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_candidate_state_id value = great_depression_center_best_state_id compare = less_than }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_best_score = great_depression_center_candidate_score }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_best_state_id = great_depression_center_candidate_state_id }
				save_event_target_as = great_depression_center_candidate_state
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_candidate_saved = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_candidate_saved value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals } }
			event_target:great_depression_center_candidate_state = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_registry_index = ROOT.great_depression_center_count }
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_episode_id = ROOT.great_depression_active_episode_id }
				ROOT = {
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_center_candidate_state }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_stage_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project.none }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries value = building_level@industrial_complex }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries value = building_level@arms_factory }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.none }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_start_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_due_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_pause_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_owner_country_entries value = ROOT }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				}
				set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_distressed }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_count value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_registration_failed = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_count value = great_depression_required_center_count compare = less_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_registration_failed = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
}

great_depression_apply_event34_snapshot_adjustment = {
	# Event 34's existing supplier adapter already supplies the final
	# Overheating anchor. The remaining adjustment is derived from the frozen
	# handoff fields and is clamped before it can affect public Severity.
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_snapshot_final_overheating
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_inherited_overheating_pressure }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_overheating = great_depression_call_snapshot_final_overheating }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_overheating = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.final_overheating_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_overheating }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_snapshot_peak_overheating
			has_variable = great_depression_call_snapshot_final_overheating
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_peak_excess = great_depression_call_snapshot_peak_overheating }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_peak_excess = great_depression_call_snapshot_final_overheating }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_peak_excess value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_peak_excess = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.peak_overheating_weight }
			add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_peak_excess }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_landing_state
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_landing_state value = constant:great_depression_event34_snapshot_state.failed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.landing_failed_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_landing_state
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_landing_state value = constant:great_depression_event34_snapshot_state.prepared compare = equals }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.landing_prepared_relief }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_reserve_units
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_reserve_units value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.reserve_available_relief }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_reserve_units }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.reserve_depleted_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_project_units
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_project_units value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.active_project_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_region_units
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_region_units value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_region_pressure = great_depression_call_event34_region_units }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_region_pressure = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.region_pressure }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_region_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_shock_units
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_shock_units value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_shock_pressure = great_depression_call_event34_shock_units }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_shock_pressure = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.shock_pressure }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_shock_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_repeat_history_count
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_repeat_history_count value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_repeat_pressure = great_depression_call_event34_repeat_history_count }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_repeat_pressure = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_repeat_pressure = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.repeat_history_pressure }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_repeat_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_dangerous_days
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_dangerous_days value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_dangerous_pressure = great_depression_call_event34_dangerous_days }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_dangerous_pressure = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.dangerous_days_weight }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_dangerous_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment value = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.maximum_adjustment compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.maximum_adjustment }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_floor = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_floor = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.maximum_adjustment }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment value = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_floor compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_floor }
	}
	add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_inherited_snapshot_adjustment }
}

great_depression_resolve_initial_severity = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.independent }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_input_mode value = constant:great_depression_severity_input.starting_severity compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_call_starting_severity }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.stable_independent compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.stable_independent }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.fragile_independent compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.fragile_independent }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.inherited_baseline compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.inherited_contagion compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.inherited_social compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.inherited_worldwide compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.baseline }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.evolution_contagion_delta }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.evolution_social_delta }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_inherited_snapshot.evolution_worldwide_delta }
		}
		great_depression_apply_event34_snapshot_adjustment = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.contagion_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.contagion_conversion }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.worldwide_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.worldwide_conversion }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_profile value = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.relapse compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_severity_seed.relapse }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_inherited_overheating_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_inherited_overheating_pressure_value }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_inherited_overheating_pressure
		clear_variable = great_depression_inherited_overheating_pressure_value
	}
}

great_depression_apply_opening_shock = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.independent_severe }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_baseline_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_baseline_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_speculative_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_speculative_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_miracle_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_miracle_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_runaway_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_runaway_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.contagion_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.contagion_conversion }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.worldwide_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.worldwide_conversion }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.relapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.relapse }
	}
	add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_opening_shock_value }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_opening_shock }
	set_country_flag = great_depression_opening_shock_receipt
	set_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_value = great_depression_opening_shock_value }
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_expiry = global.num_days }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_expiry = constant:great_depression_event.opening_shock_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_opening_shock_until = great_depression_opening_shock_expiry }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_opening_shock_applied = constant:great_depression_api.one }
}

great_depression_clamp_public_severity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_depression_severity
				value = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_minimum
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_minimum }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_depression_severity
				value = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_maximum
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_maximum }
	}
}

great_depression_set_phase_from_severity = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.economic_paralysis }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.depression }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.stabilization_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.depression }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_floor compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.stabilization }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_phase_changed_date = global.num_days }
}

great_depression_refresh_national_modifier = {
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_panic }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_depression }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_deep }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_systemic }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_paralysis }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_stabilizing }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_recovery }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_financial_contagion }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_social_collapse_pressure }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.panic_and_contraction compare = equals } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_panic }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.depression compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_systemic }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.deep_depression_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_deep }
		}
		else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_depression } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.stabilization compare = equals } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_stabilizing }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery compare = equals } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_recovery }
	}
	else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_paralysis } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_financial_contagion }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_social_collapse_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
	}
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Public state, source receipts, and external-surface adapters
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

	great_depression_incident_clear_episode_state = yes
	great_depression_clear_outgoing_contagion_network = yes
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_civilian_commitment_major }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_panic }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_depression }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_deep }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_systemic }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_paralysis }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_stabilizing }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_national_recovery }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_opening_shock }

	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_social_collapse_pressure }

	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }

	clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_relationship_entries

	clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_stage_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_relationship_entries

	clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_containment_source_count
	clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_required_target_count
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_network_initialized
}

great_depression_apply_evolution_floor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_evolution_floor
				value = great_depression_call_evolution_floor
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_evolution_floor = great_depression_call_evolution_floor }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.event_id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
		add_ideas = great_depression_economic_contagion
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_network_initialized } }
			great_depression_initialize_contagion_network = yes
			set_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_network_initialized
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
		add_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
	}
	great_depression_record_enabled_evolution_milestones = yes
}

# COUNTRY scope. Record each enabled Event 35 evolution once through the
# shared events-log evolution owner. Setting the evolution floor itself never
# changes Chaos; only the concrete outcome adapters elsewhere do so.
great_depression_record_enabled_evolution_milestones = {
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.event_id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.evolution_type }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_tier }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression_logged }
		}
		set_global_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression_logged
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		save_event_target_as = events_log_evolution_actor
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Evolution activation is delayed through the owner MTTH
# entries and requires the matching Chaos tier and settings context. Raising
# an evolution floor does not change Chaos; only concrete outcome adapters do.
great_depression_try_evolution_activation = {
	if = {
		limit = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.event_id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.financial_contagion_tier }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = less_than }
				is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_due_day } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_evolution_delay = mtth:great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_interval }
				set_variable = { great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_due_day = global.num_days }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_due_day = great_depression_evolution_delay }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_due_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				great_depression_apply_evolution_floor = yes
				great_depression_apply_active_ideas = yes
				set_variable = { great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_day = global.num_days }
				clear_variable = great_depression_financial_contagion_activation_due_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_floor
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_proof
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.social_collapse_tier }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_social_collapse_activation_due_day } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_evolution_delay = mtth:great_depression_social_collapse_activation_interval }
				set_variable = { great_depression_social_collapse_activation_due_day = global.num_days }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_social_collapse_activation_due_day = great_depression_evolution_delay }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_social_collapse_activation_due_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				great_depression_apply_evolution_floor = yes
				great_depression_apply_active_ideas = yes
				set_variable = { great_depression_social_collapse_activation_day = global.num_days }
				clear_variable = great_depression_social_collapse_activation_due_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_floor
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_proof
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:great_depression_evolution_log.second_great_depression_tier }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = less_than }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				is_current_evolution_enabled = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_due_day } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_evolution_delay = mtth:great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_interval }
				set_variable = { great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_due_day = global.num_days }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_due_day = great_depression_evolution_delay }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_due_day compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				great_depression_apply_evolution_floor = yes
				great_depression_apply_active_ideas = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_ai = no
						has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
						NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated }
					}
					set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_candidate
				}
				great_depression_activate_worldwide_episode = yes
				set_variable = { great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_day = global.num_days }
				clear_variable = great_depression_second_great_depression_activation_due_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_floor
				clear_variable = great_depression_call_evolution_proof
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_set_qualitative_band = {
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_recovery
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_stabilization
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_fragile
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_depression
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_deep
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_systemic
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_band_paralysis
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.paralysis_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_paralysis
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.systemic_breakdown_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_systemic
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.deep_depression_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_deep
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.depression_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_depression
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.fragile_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_fragile
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_public_band.stabilizing_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_stabilization
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = great_depression_band_recovery
	}
}

great_depression_apply_active_ideas = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active }
		add_ideas = great_depression_active
	}
	else = {
		remove_ideas = great_depression_active
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_ideas = great_depression_economic_contagion
	}
	else = {
		remove_ideas = great_depression_economic_contagion
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
	}
	else = {
		remove_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
	}
}

great_depression_record_source_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_receipt_is_duplicate = yes } }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_receipt_ids value = great_depression_call_receipt_id }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_source_events value = great_depression_call_source_event_id }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_source_episodes value = great_depression_call_source_episode_id }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_callers value = great_depression_call_caller_type }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_pacing_modes value = great_depression_call_pacing_mode }
		add_to_array = { array = great_depression_committed_history_modes value = great_depression_call_history_mode }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_receipt_id = great_depression_call_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_source_event_id = great_depression_call_source_event_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_source_episode_id = great_depression_call_source_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_caller_type = great_depression_call_caller_type }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_episode_receipt_count }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_episode_receipt_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_episode_receipt_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_record_history = {
	set_variable = { global.last_fired_event_id = constant:great_depression_api.event_35_id }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_payload_override_active = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_payload_override = great_depression_depression_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_actor_override_active = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_temp_variable = { events_log_history_actor_override = THIS }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.events_log_history_sequence }
		record_events_log_history_entry = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_history_recorded = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
}

great_depression_queue_external_surfaces = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.base }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.boom_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.financial_contagion_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.financial_contagion }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.worldwide_depression_conversion compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.worldwide_contraction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
				has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse
			}
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.social_collapse }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.post_recovery_relapse compare = equals }
				has_country_flag = great_depression_relapse_active
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_report_variant = constant:great_depression_report_variant.recovery }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_report_policy value = constant:great_depression_report_policy.queue compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_report_policy value = constant:great_depression_report_policy.force compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_report_pending
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_report_queued = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr35.2 days = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_news_policy value = constant:great_depression_news_policy.queue compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_news_policy value = constant:great_depression_news_policy.force compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_news_pending
	}
}

great_depression_store_call_context = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_source_event_id = great_depression_call_source_event_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_source_episode_id = great_depression_call_source_episode_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_history_mode = great_depression_call_history_mode }
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_source_caller = great_depression_call_caller_type }
	set_variable = { great_depression_active_start_date = global.num_days }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = great_depression_call_source_country }
		event_target:great_depression_call_source_country = {
			ROOT = { save_event_target_as = great_depression_active_source_country }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_episode_id = great_depression_call_source_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_collapse_receipt_id = great_depression_call_snapshot_collapse_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_final_overheating = great_depression_call_snapshot_final_overheating }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_peak_overheating = great_depression_call_snapshot_peak_overheating }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_landing_state = great_depression_call_event34_landing_state }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_reserve_units = great_depression_call_event34_reserve_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_project_units = great_depression_call_event34_project_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_region_units = great_depression_call_event34_region_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_shock_units = great_depression_call_event34_shock_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_repeat_history_count = great_depression_call_event34_repeat_history_count }
		set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_dangerous_days = great_depression_call_event34_dangerous_days }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_call_event34_collapse_day }
			set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_collapse_day = great_depression_call_event34_collapse_day }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_inherited_event34_collapse_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_clear_center_resolution_receipts = {
	every_controlled_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_receipt
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_completed_receipt
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed_receipt
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
			clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_receipt
			clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_completed_receipt
			clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed_receipt
			clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt
		}
	}
}

great_depression_clear_registered_center_runtime = {
	# Recovery and transaction rollback must also clean a registered state that
	# changed hands. A controlled-state scan alone cannot reach that state.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_center_cleanup_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_center_cleanup_index = {
				great_depression_clear_center_state_flags = yes
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_loss_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works_completed_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_completed_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_failed_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_deliberate_liquidation_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_contract_open
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_adopted_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_humanitarian_famine_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_humanitarian_migration_receipt
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_occupation_context_observed
				clr_state_flag = great_depression_inherited_center
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_type
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_state
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_stage
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_start_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_stage_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_due_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_pause_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_pause_days
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_work_days
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_paralysis_days
				clear_variable = great_depression_center_project_last_process_day
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Freezes the achievement owner, opening episode, original
# center registry, and factory baselines at the accepted start transaction.
# Debug/force/test entries remain playable but can never begin mastery proof.
great_depression_initialize_achievement_tracking = {
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated
	clear_array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_state_ids
	clear_array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_civilian_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_military_entries
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_owner_country_id = THIS.id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_opening_severity = great_depression_episode_start_severity }
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_opening_center_count = great_depression_episode_start_center_count }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_index = {
				save_event_target_as = great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_state
				ROOT = {
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_state }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_civilian_entries value = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries^great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_index }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_achievement_original_center_military_entries value = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries^great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_index }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	clear_event_target = great_depression_achievement_center_freeze_state
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = yes
				is_debug = yes
				has_country_flag = chaosx_test_country_initialized
				has_country_flag = force_trigger_mode_enabled
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.debug_or_test compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_pacing_mode value = constant:great_depression_pacing.debug_no_pacing compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_report_policy value = constant:great_depression_report_policy.force compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_news_policy value = constant:great_depression_news_policy.force compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated
	}
	else = {
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_opening_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
		set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_every_center_reopened_candidate
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.independent_event_35 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_episode_start_severity value = constant:great_depression_achievement.severe_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_back_to_work_candidate
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				capital_scope = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_long_fall_candidate
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_candidate
		}
	}
}

great_depression_update_achievement_continuity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
			OR = {
				is_ai = yes
				is_subject = yes
				is_debug = yes
				has_country_flag = chaosx_test_country_initialized
				has_country_flag = force_trigger_mode_enabled
				NOT = { great_depression_has_viable_economy = yes }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = THIS.id value = great_depression_achievement_owner_country_id compare = equals } }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_episode_id value = great_depression_achievement_episode_id compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated
	}
}

great_depression_refresh_achievement_center_proof = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_original_centers_positive = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_all_centers_positive = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_achievement_original_center_state_ids^num }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_achievement_original_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_achievement_original_center_check_index
			var:great_depression_achievement_original_center_state_ids^great_depression_achievement_original_center_check_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
							NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_restructured
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
								}
							}
						}
					}
					ROOT = { set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_original_centers_positive = constant:great_depression_api.zero } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_achievement_all_center_check_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_achievement_all_center_check_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT }
							NOT = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated
							NOT = {
								OR = {
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_restructured
									has_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
								}
							}
						}
					}
					ROOT = { set_variable = { great_depression_achievement_all_centers_positive = constant:great_depression_api.zero } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
