		}
		great_depression_queue_external_surfaces = yes
		great_depression_record_history = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
			}
			great_depression_activate_worldwide_episode = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals }
			}
			great_depression_apply_supplier_collapse = yes
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_new_crisis }
	}
}

great_depression_resolve_deepening_amount = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_event.deepening_minimum }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_severity_input_mode value = constant:great_depression_severity_input.deepen_amount compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = great_depression_call_deepen_amount }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_baseline_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_baseline_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_speculative_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_speculative_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_miracle_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_miracle_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.event_34_runaway_collapse compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.event_34_runaway_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile value = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.active_crisis_deepening compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_shock_strength.active_crisis_deepening }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_deepening_value value = constant:great_depression_event.deepening_maximum compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_deepening_value = constant:great_depression_event.deepening_maximum }
	}
}

great_depression_deepen_active_crisis = {
	set_country_flag = great_depression_transition_pending
	set_variable = { great_depression_previous_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
	great_depression_resolve_deepening_amount = yes
	add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_deepening_value }
	great_depression_apply_evolution_floor = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
		}
		great_depression_activate_worldwide_episode = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_caller_type value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals }
		}
		great_depression_apply_supplier_collapse = yes
	}
	great_depression_clamp_public_severity = yes
	set_variable = { great_depression_episode_peak_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
	set_variable = { great_depression_severity_trend = great_depression_deepening_value }
	great_depression_set_qualitative_band = yes
	great_depression_apply_active_ideas = yes
	great_depression_refresh_national_modifier = yes
	great_depression_store_call_context = yes
	great_depression_record_source_receipt = yes
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_transition_pending
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepened = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_active_evolution = great_depression_evolution_floor }
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_final_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
	great_depression_queue_external_surfaces = yes
	great_depression_record_history = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_deepening_value value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_merge_without_severity_change }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_deepening }
	}
}

great_depression_start_or_deepen = {
	great_depression_initialize_global_runtime = yes
	great_depression_initialize_country_runtime = yes
	great_depression_reset_call_outputs = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_contract_is_valid = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_missing_contract }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.missing_contract }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_has_viable_economy = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_invalid_economy }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.no_viable_state }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_caller_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_invalid_caller }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_caller }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_source_context_is_valid = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_missing_source_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.missing_source_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_receipt_ledger_has_capacity = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.receipt_ledger_full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { great_depression_call_receipt_is_duplicate = yes }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.duplicate_receipt }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.duplicate_receipt }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_no_op = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_active_episode_id }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_final_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_active_evolution = great_depression_evolution_floor }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_pacing_mode_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_history_mode_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_report_policy_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_news_policy_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor_is_valid = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_invalid_evolution_floor }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_evolution_floor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_severity_input_is_valid = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_conflicting_severity_inputs }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_severity_input }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_shock_profile_is_known = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_opening_shock_is_compatible = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_context }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_pacing_is_compatible = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.incompatible_active_state }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
			NOT = { great_depression_active_country_registry_has_room = yes }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.invalid_context }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.active_country_registry_full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_active_target_is_valid = yes } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_incompatible_active_state }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.active_target }
		}
		else = {
			great_depression_deepen_active_crisis = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { great_depression_call_new_crisis_target_is_valid = yes } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_result = constant:great_depression_result.rejected_invalid_country }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_reject_reason = constant:great_depression_reject_reason.invalid_country }
	}
	else = {
		great_depression_initialize_new_crisis = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_result value = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_new_crisis compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_result value = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_deepening compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_result value = constant:great_depression_result.accepted_merge_without_severity_change compare = equals }
			}
		}
		great_depression_reset_global_recovery_proof = yes
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_call_final_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
	great_depression_clear_public_call_inputs = yes
}

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Bounded active-country lifecycle
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

great_depression_clear_opening_shock = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_opening_shock_receipt
			has_variable = great_depression_opening_shock_until
			check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_opening_shock_until compare = greater_than }
		}
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_opening_shock_receipt
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_opening_shock }
		clear_variable = great_depression_opening_shock_until
	}
}

great_depression_update_phase_hysteresis = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_phase } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.panic_and_contraction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_receipt } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.economic_paralysis }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.panic_and_contraction compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_active_start_date compare = greater_than }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_panic_elapsed = global.num_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_panic_elapsed = great_depression_active_start_date }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_panic_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.panic_minimum_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.stabilization_floor compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.depression }
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.stabilization }
			}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.depression compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.stabilization_floor compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_stabilization_below_days = constant:great_depression_event.pulse_days }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_stabilization_below_days value = constant:great_depression_event.stabilization_proof_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.stabilization }
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_stabilization_below_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.stabilization compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_floor compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_recovery_below_days = constant:great_depression_event.pulse_days }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_below_days value = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_proof_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.relapse_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.depression }
			set_variable = { great_depression_recovery_below_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_recovery_below_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.economic_paralysis compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = less_than } }
			great_depression_set_phase_from_severity = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery compare = equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.relapse_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_phase = constant:great_depression_phase.depression }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_relapse_active
		}
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_phase_changed_date = global.num_days }
}

# STATE scope. Record physical factory loss in both the national receipt and
# the indexed center ledger before any later cleanup can erase the state.
great_depression_record_center_factory_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_registry_index
			is_owned_by = ROOT
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_loss_index = great_depression_center_registry_index }
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = great_depression_event35_civilian_loss_total }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_event35_civilian_loss_total = great_depression_center_factory_loss_amount }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_event35_civilian_loss_total = great_depression_center_factory_loss_amount }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries^great_depression_center_loss_index = great_depression_center_factory_loss_amount }
		}
		clear_temp_variable = great_depression_center_loss_index
		clear_temp_variable = great_depression_center_factory_loss_amount
	}
}

great_depression_apply_sustained_center_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_breakdown_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_event.breakdown_warning_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				num_of_civilian_factories > constant:great_depression_api.minimum_viable_factory_count
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification }
			is_owned_by = ROOT
			is_controlled_by = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
			}
			NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_loss_receipt }
			industrial_complex > constant:great_depression_api.minimum_usable_factory_count
		}
		great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
		remove_building = {
			type = industrial_complex
			level = constant:great_depression_scaling.state_loss_factory_cap
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_center_factory_loss_amount = constant:great_depression_scaling.state_loss_factory_cap }
		great_depression_record_center_factory_loss = yes
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_loss_receipt
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned
		set_state_flag = great_depression_center_scarred
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.scarred }
		set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.scarred }
		great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_hollowed }
		ROOT = {
			set_country_flag = great_depression_material_center_loss
			set_variable = { great_depression_last_center_loss_day = global.num_days }
		}
		great_depression_submit_humanitarian_center_pressure = yes
	}
}

great_depression_expire_center_protection = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected
			has_variable = great_depression_center_protection_expiry_day
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_center_protection_expiry_day
				value = global.num_days
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_protected }
		clr_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected
		clear_variable = great_depression_center_protection_expiry_day
	}
}

great_depression_update_registered_centers = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_center_count }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_center_update_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_center_update_index = {
				great_depression_expire_center_protection = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification }
						is_owned_by = ROOT
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_breakdown_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_action.center_idled_after_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
					}
					great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
					set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled }
					set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled }
					great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_idled }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused }
						NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification }
						is_owned_by = ROOT
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						ROOT = {
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_breakdown_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_action.center_shuttered_after_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
					}
					great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
					set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
					set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
					great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
				}
				great_depression_apply_sustained_center_failure = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_protected_center_active
				NOT = { any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = ROOT has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected } }
			}
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_protected_center_active
		}
	}
}

great_depression_update_center_resolution_count = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_centers_resolved_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_center_resolution_scan_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_center_resolution_scan_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_positive_resolution_receipt
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_receipt
						}
					}
					ROOT = { add_to_variable = { great_depression_centers_resolved_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_register_breakdown_warning = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_breakdown_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_event.breakdown_warning_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_warning }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_warning
		set_variable = { great_depression_breakdown_warning_day = global.num_days }
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr35.3 days = 1 }
	}
}

great_depression_apply_maximum_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_paralysis_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_proof_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_opening_shock_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_receipt }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_receipt
		set_variable = { great_depression_maximum_emergency_day = global.num_days }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_active
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_consequence_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. A missed maximum-severity response records one bounded local
# failure when a usable, unprotected center exists. Shuttering is the failure
# consequence; physical factory loss remains owned by the sustained-center
# failure or deliberate-liquidation contracts.
great_depression_apply_maximum_failure_center_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_center_failure_receipt }
			great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = great_depression_center
				NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed }
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
				}
				industrial_complex > constant:great_depression_api.zero
			}
			ROOT = { set_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_center_failure_receipt }
			great_depression_clear_center_physical_stage = yes
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_ledger_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
			great_depression_write_center_stage_ledger = yes
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
		}
	}
}

# Rebuild the hidden causal components from current conditions before each
# severity pulse. The player never sees these component values. They keep
# credit, demand, trade, industry, fiscal capacity, social order, and wartime
# strain responsive to the usable economy and to the bounded center registry.
great_depression_rebuild_hidden_components = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_war_stress = constant:great_depression_api.zero }

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_collapsed }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.credit_supplier_risk }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.credit_supplier_risk }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_event34_inherited }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.credit_boom_inheritance }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_policy_whiplash_active }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.credit_policy_whiplash }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_bank_audit_complete
				has_country_flag = great_depression_viable_institutions_recapitalized
				has_country_flag = great_depression_durable_credit_guarantee
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_credit_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.credit_audit_relief }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_employment_guarantee_active }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_public_works_relief }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_employment_compact
				has_country_flag = great_depression_reconstruction_employment_fund
				has_country_flag = great_depression_public_works_project_active
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_public_works_relief }
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_hidden_center_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_hidden_center_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_distressed_center }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_idled_center }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_shuttered_center }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_abandoned_center }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_hollowed_center }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_public_works }
					ROOT = { add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_public_works_relief } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_reopened
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_protected
						}
					}
					ROOT = {
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_demand_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.demand_reopened_relief }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_recovery_relief }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_collapsed
				has_country_flag = great_depression_event34_supplier_pressure
				has_country_flag = great_depression_import_contract_shifted
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.trade_failure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.trade_supplier_risk }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.trade_global_contraction }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_clearing_agreement_active
				has_country_flag = great_depression_clearing_bloc_member
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.trade_clearing_relief }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_freight_secured }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_trade_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.trade_freight_relief }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_event34_inherited }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_inherited_region }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_event34_supplier_pressure }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_center_pressure }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_essential_production_restored
				has_country_flag = great_depression_strategic_network_designated
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_recovery_relief }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_freight_secured }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.industrial_recovery_relief }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_policy_whiplash_active }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.fiscal_policy_whiplash }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_emergency_budget_balanced
				has_country_flag = great_depression_public_obligations_restructured
				has_country_flag = great_depression_ordinary_budget_restored
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.fiscal_response }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_reserves_reserved }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.fiscal_reserves }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects < constant:great_depression_event.minimum_usable_civilian_factories }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.fiscal_capacity_shortage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_successful_action_count
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_successful_action_count value = constant:great_depression_action.second_action_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.fiscal_response }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.social_low_stability }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_medium_stability_floor }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.social_medium_stability }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_national_unrest
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.social_incident }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_response_failed
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_government_crisis
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.social_failed_response }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_strike_negotiated
				has_country_flag = great_depression_councils_recognized
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_government_settled
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_social_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.social_compact_relief }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_freight_secured }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_essential_production_restored }
			}
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_war_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.war_supply_shortage }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_war_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.war_stability_pressure }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_freight_secured
				has_country_flag = great_depression_essential_production_restored
			}
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_war_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.war_supply_relief }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_stability > constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_high_stability_floor }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_hidden_war_stress = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.war_stability_relief }
		}
	}

	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_credit_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_demand_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_trade_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_industrial_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_fiscal_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_social_stress }
	add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_value = great_depression_hidden_war_stress }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_hidden_net_value
		min = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.minimum
		max = constant:great_depression_hidden_component.maximum
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_hidden_net_pressure = great_depression_hidden_net_value }
}

great_depression_weekly_pulse = {
	if = {
		limit = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
		great_depression_refresh_public_thresholds = yes
		great_depression_set_pulse_interval = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_last_pulse_day } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_last_pulse_day = global.num_days }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_elapsed = great_depression_last_pulse_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_pulse_elapsed value = great_depression_pulse_interval compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_last_pulse_day = global.num_days }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_days_applied = great_depression_pulse_elapsed }
			great_depression_rebuild_hidden_components = yes
			set_variable = { great_depression_previous_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.panic_and_contraction compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.panic_pulse_delta }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.emergency_public_works compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.public_works_pulse_delta }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.rescue_strategic_industry compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.strategic_pulse_delta }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.stabilize_finance_and_trade compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.finance_pulse_delta }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.austerity_and_retrenchment compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.austerity_pulse_delta }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.direct_state_planning compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.state_planning_pulse_delta }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.let_the_market_clear compare = equals } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.market_clear_pulse_delta }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_protected_center_active }
				add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.protected_pulse_relief }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
				add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.incident_pulse_pressure }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_exposure_open }
				add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_action.contagion_pulse_pressure }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_incident_pressure_active }
				add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_incident_timing.pressure_pulse }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
					has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
					OR = {
						check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.shock compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.contraction compare = equals }
						check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination compare = equals }
					}
				}
				add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_scaling.global_pressure_factor }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = great_depression_hidden_net_pressure }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_pulse_delta value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
					has_variable = great_depression_successful_action_count
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_successful_action_count value = constant:great_depression_action.third_action_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_scaling.diminishing_return_third }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_successful_action_count value = constant:great_depression_action.second_action_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_scaling.diminishing_return_second }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_pulse_delta value = constant:great_depression_event.severity_pulse_minimum compare = less_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_event.severity_pulse_minimum }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_pulse_delta value = constant:great_depression_event.severity_pulse_maximum compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_pulse_delta = constant:great_depression_event.severity_pulse_maximum }
			}
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_depression_severity = great_depression_pulse_delta }
			great_depression_clamp_public_severity = yes
			set_variable = { great_depression_severity_trend = great_depression_pulse_delta }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = great_depression_episode_peak_severity compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_episode_peak_severity = great_depression_depression_severity }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_breakdown_severe_days = great_depression_pulse_days_applied }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_breakdown_severe_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_paralysis_severe_days = great_depression_pulse_days_applied }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_paralysis_severe_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			}
			great_depression_try_evolution_activation = yes
			great_depression_process_contagion_network = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor
					NOT = { has_civil_war = yes }
				}
					add_to_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_days = great_depression_pulse_days_applied }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_days = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			}
			great_depression_update_phase_hysteresis = yes
			great_depression_process_center_transfers = yes
			great_depression_process_center_projects = yes
			great_depression_update_registered_centers = yes
			great_depression_update_center_resolution_count = yes
			great_depression_activate_social_collapse = yes
			great_depression_progress_social_ladder = yes
			great_depression_run_due_incident_pool = yes
			great_depression_register_breakdown_warning = yes
			great_depression_process_national_breakdown = yes
			great_depression_apply_maximum_consequence = yes
			great_depression_clear_opening_shock = yes
			great_depression_set_qualitative_band = yes
			great_depression_apply_active_ideas = yes
			great_depression_refresh_national_modifier = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery compare = equals } }
				great_depression_try_complete_recovery = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_process_active_registry = {
	great_depression_initialize_global_runtime = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_active_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_active_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_active_countries^num
			value = great_depression_active_registry_index
			var:global.great_depression_active_countries^great_depression_active_registry_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
					great_depression_update_achievement_continuity = yes
					great_depression_refresh_mission_capacity_ledger = yes
					great_depression_weekly_pulse = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_register_recovery_watch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries value = THIS } }
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^num
					value = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_watch_country_cap
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry = global.num_days }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_watch_days }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_episode_entries value = great_depression_active_episode_id }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry_entries value = great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active
	}
}

great_depression_start_relapse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			great_depression_has_viable_economy = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_relapse_cooldown }
			has_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active
			has_variable = great_depression_prior_completed_episode_id
			OR = {
				has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor
				has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
			}
		}
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_contract_supplied = constant:great_depression_api.contract_version }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_caller_type = constant:great_depression_caller.post_recovery_relapse }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_event_id = constant:great_depression_api.event_35_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_episode_id = great_depression_prior_completed_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_pacing_mode = constant:great_depression_pacing.conversion_no_pacing }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_history_mode = constant:great_depression_history_mode.relapse }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_report_policy = constant:great_depression_report_policy.queue }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_news_policy = constant:great_depression_news_policy.queue }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.baseline }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_last_recovery_evolution }
			set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = great_depression_last_recovery_evolution }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_input_mode = constant:great_depression_severity_input.severity_profile }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.relapse }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.relapse }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_relapse_prior_episode_id = great_depression_prior_completed_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_relapse_prior_completion_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_relapse_window_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_relapse_scar_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		set_variable = { great_depression_call_relapse_profile = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.relapse }
		great_depression_start_or_deepen = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_started value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_deepened value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_relapse_cooldown_duration = constant:great_depression_event.relapse_guard_days }
			set_country_flag = { flag = great_depression_relapse_cooldown days = great_depression_relapse_cooldown_duration }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_relapse_active
		}
	}
}

great_depression_process_recovery_watch = {
	great_depression_initialize_global_runtime = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^num
			value = great_depression_recovery_watch_index
			var:global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^great_depression_recovery_watch_index = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor
								has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
							}
						}
						great_depression_start_relapse = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = {
							check_variable = {
								var = global.num_days
								value = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry_entries^great_depression_recovery_watch_index
								compare = greater_than_or_equals
							}
						}
						clr_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	great_depression_prune_recovery_watch = yes
}

# Remove expired or consumed recovery-watch rows from the aligned global
# arrays. Reverse traversal keeps an index valid when a row is removed and
# prevents the bounded registry from filling with inert history.
great_depression_prune_recovery_watch = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^num }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index > constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		var:global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries^great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_watch_active } }
				ROOT = {
					remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_countries index = ROOT.great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index }
					remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_episode_entries index = ROOT.great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index }
					remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_recovery_watch_expiry_entries index = ROOT.great_depression_recovery_watch_prune_index }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_register_refire_memory = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_prior_completed_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_prior_completion_day = global.num_days }
	set_variable = { great_depression_prior_completion_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_recovery_evolution = great_depression_evolution_floor }
	set_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_completed
	set_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_scar_memory
	set_variable = { great_depression_refire_safeguard_duration = constant:great_depression_event.refire_safeguard_days }
	set_country_flag = { flag = great_depression_refire_safeguard_active value = 1 days = great_depression_refire_safeguard_duration }
	great_depression_register_recovery_watch = yes
}

great_depression_add_recovery_legacy = {
	# A country carries one doctrine legacy at a time. Replace the prior
	# doctrine and scar marker so relapse/recovery never stacks stale ideas.
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_public_works
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_strategic_industry
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_finance_and_trade
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_austerity
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_direct_state_planning
	remove_ideas = great_depression_legacy_market_clear
	remove_ideas = great_depression_scar_hollow_recovery
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.emergency_public_works compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_public_works
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.rescue_strategic_industry compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_strategic_industry
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.stabilize_finance_and_trade compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_finance_and_trade
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.austerity_and_retrenchment compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_austerity
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.direct_state_planning compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_direct_state_planning
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.let_the_market_clear compare = equals } }
		add_ideas = great_depression_legacy_market_clear
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_material_center_loss }
		add_ideas = great_depression_scar_hollow_recovery
	}
}

great_depression_freeze_episode_summary = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_start_severity = great_depression_episode_start_severity }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_peak_severity = great_depression_episode_peak_severity }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_center_count = great_depression_episode_start_center_count }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_evolution = great_depression_episode_start_evolution }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_source_event = great_depression_active_source_event_id }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_source_caller = great_depression_active_source_caller }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_episode_recovery_outcome = great_depression_recovery_outcome }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_center_restructured_count = great_depression_centers_restructured_count }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_center_liquidated_count = great_depression_centers_liquidated_count }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_civilian_factory_levels_lost = great_depression_event35_civilian_loss_total }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_military_factory_levels_lost = great_depression_event35_military_loss_total }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_center_transfer_count = great_depression_center_transfer_count }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_contagion_conversion_count = great_depression_contagion_conversion_count }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event35_chaos_increase = great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event35_chaos_relief = great_depression_event35_chaos_relief_total }
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_relapse_active = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_relapse_active }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_relapse_active = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_state_ids
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_stage_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_project_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_loss_receipt_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_civilian_loss_entries
	clear_array = great_depression_last_completed_center_military_loss_entries
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_state_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_freeze_center_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_freeze_center_index = {
				save_event_target_as = great_depression_freeze_center_state
				ROOT = {
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_freeze_center_state }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_stage_entries value = great_depression_center_stage_entries^great_depression_freeze_center_index }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_project_entries value = great_depression_center_project_entries^great_depression_freeze_center_index }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_loss_receipt_entries value = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries^great_depression_freeze_center_index }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_civilian_loss_entries value = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries^great_depression_freeze_center_index }
					add_to_array = { array = great_depression_last_completed_center_military_loss_entries value = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries^great_depression_freeze_center_index }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	clear_event_target = great_depression_freeze_center_state
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = great_depression_inherited_event34_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_episode_id = great_depression_inherited_event34_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_collapse_receipt_id = great_depression_inherited_event34_collapse_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_final_overheating = great_depression_inherited_event34_final_overheating }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_peak_overheating = great_depression_inherited_event34_peak_overheating }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_landing_state = great_depression_inherited_event34_landing_state }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_reserve_units = great_depression_inherited_event34_reserve_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_project_units = great_depression_inherited_event34_project_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_region_units = great_depression_inherited_event34_region_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_shock_units = great_depression_inherited_event34_shock_units }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_repeat_history_count = great_depression_inherited_event34_repeat_history_count }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_dangerous_days = great_depression_inherited_event34_dangerous_days }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_event34_collapse_day = great_depression_inherited_event34_collapse_day }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_last_completed_global_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
	}
}

great_depression_resolve_recovery_outcome = {
	set_variable = { great_depression_recovery_outcome = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.uneven }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_material_center_loss
				has_country_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_disqualifier
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_liquidated_count value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
				any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned }
				any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_recovery_outcome = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.hollow }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_reopened_count value = great_depression_episode_start_center_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered } }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned } }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed } }
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_recovery_outcome = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.strong }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_last_recovery_result = great_depression_recovery_outcome }
}

# COUNTRY scope. This evaluator is called both from national recovery and from
# the bounded global contributor registry, so a stable supplier that never
# converted into Event 35 can satisfy the same worldwide reconstruction proof.
great_depression_evaluate_recovery_of_nations_achievement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_candidate
			has_variable = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_episode_id
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals }
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_stage
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_secured
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_contribution_count value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_contribution_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_contribution_receipt_ids^num value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_contribution_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_country_flag = great_depression_global_recipient_improved
			has_country_flag = great_depression_global_major_recipient_improved
			has_stability > constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }
			is_subject = no
			great_depression_has_viable_economy = yes
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = great_depression_global_national_recovery_proven
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_national_recovery_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals }
				}
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_episode_id }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals } }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_recovery_of_nations_proven
	}
}

great_depression_evaluate_global_reconstruction_contributors = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num
			value = great_depression_reconstruction_achievement_index
			var:global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^great_depression_reconstruction_achievement_index = {
				great_depression_evaluate_recovery_of_nations_achievement = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_evaluate_achievements = {
	# Proof is frozen before active state is cleared. These flags are durable
	# receipts; the public Severity and centre ledgers are not exposed after
	# recovery.
	great_depression_update_achievement_continuity = yes
	great_depression_refresh_achievement_center_proof = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_back_to_work_candidate
			has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated }
			is_ai = no
			is_subject = no
			check_variable = { var = THIS.id value = great_depression_achievement_owner_country_id compare = equals }

			check_variable = { var = great_depression_episode_start_severity value = constant:great_depression_achievement.severe_start compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_outcome value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.strong compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_outcome value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.uneven compare = equals }
			}

			check_variable = { var = great_depression_event35_military_loss_total value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }

			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_disqualifier }

	if = {

				check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_outcome value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.uneven compare = equals }
			}
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_episode_start_center_count value = great_depression_required_center_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_resolved_count value = great_depression_center_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }

			check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_liquidated_count value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_center_transfer_disqualifier }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered } }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_abandoned } }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_hollowed } }
			NOT = { any_controlled_state = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center_liquidated } }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {

			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated }
			is_ai = no
			is_subject = no
			check_variable = { var = THIS.id value = great_depression_achievement_owner_country_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_episode_id value = great_depression_achievement_episode_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_doctrine value = constant:great_depression_doctrine.let_the_market_clear compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_liquidation_route_episode_id value = great_depression_active_episode_id compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_liquidation_route_switch_count value = great_depression_doctrine_switch_count compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_liquidated_count value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_liquidation_factory_levels_lost value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_event35_civilian_loss_total value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_event35_military_loss_total value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }

			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_successful }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_civil_conflict_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_civil_conflict_started }

	great_depression_evaluate_recovery_of_nations_achievement = yes

great_depression_try_complete_recovery = {
	great_depression_update_center_resolution_count = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_phase value = constant:great_depression_phase.recovery compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_recovery_below_days value = constant:great_depression_event.recovery_proof_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_centers_resolved_count value = great_depression_center_count compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_center_project_active }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_authorized
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_active_work
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_paused
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_project_certification
					}
				}
			}
		}
		great_depression_resolve_recovery_outcome = yes
		great_depression_apply_strong_recovery_chaos_relief = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_global_national_recovery_proven
			set_variable = { great_depression_global_national_recovery_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		}
		great_depression_freeze_episode_summary = yes
		great_depression_evaluate_achievements = yes
		great_depression_register_refire_memory = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_material_center_loss } }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_last_recovery_no_center_loss
		}
		great_depression_add_recovery_legacy = yes
		great_depression_clear_registered_center_runtime = yes
		great_depression_clear_center_resolution_receipts = yes
		every_controlled_state = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center }
				great_depression_clear_center_state_flags = yes
			}
		}
		clear_variable = great_depression_selected_center_state
		great_depression_unregister_active_country = yes
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_active
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_active
	clr_country_flag = great_depression_maximum_emergency_center_failure_receipt
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_warning
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_open
