		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_nonwar_outcome
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chaos_receipt
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_cabinet_restored
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_legitimacy_repaired
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_exposure_open
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_organized_actor_proof
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_major_incident_proof
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_actor_proof
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_sector_conflict
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_national_unrest

# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Event 34, Financial Contagion, Social Collapse, and global adapters
# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

great_depression_register_inherited_region = {
	# Caller may be a state scope or a country with the state target supplied.
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = great_depression_inherited_region_state }
		event_target:great_depression_inherited_region_state = {
			if = {
				limit = {
				ROOT = {
					great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_center_count value = constant:great_depression_event.center_registry_cap compare = less_than }
				}
				NOT = { has_state_flag = great_depression_center }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.distressed }
			if = {
				limit = {
					ROOT = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.financial_contagion compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_event34_project_units value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_evolution_floor value = constant:great_depression_evolution.social_collapse compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_inherited_center_stage = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_registry_index = ROOT.great_depression_center_count }
			ROOT = {
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_state_ids value = event_target:great_depression_inherited_region_state }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_stage_entries value = great_depression_inherited_center_stage }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project.none }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_loss_receipt_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_civilian_entries value = building_level@industrial_complex }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_factory_baseline_military_entries value = building_level@arms_factory }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_entries value = constant:great_depression_center_project_stage.none }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_start_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_stage_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_due_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_project_pause_day_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_owner_country_entries value = ROOT }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_civilian_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_array = { array = great_depression_center_event35_military_loss_entries value = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				add_to_variable = { great_depression_center_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_center_stage = great_depression_inherited_center_stage }
			set_state_flag = great_depression_center
			set_state_flag = great_depression_inherited_center
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_inherited_center_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.shuttered compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_shuttered }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_inherited_center_stage value = constant:great_depression_center_stage.idled compare = equals } }
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_idled }
			}
			else = {
				set_state_flag = great_depression_center_distressed
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_center_distressed }
			}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_register_supplier_risk = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries value = THIS } }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries^num }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries^num
						value = constant:great_depression_event.contagion_source_cap
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries value = THIS }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk }
			add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
	}
}

great_depression_reset_global_episode_ledgers = {
	# A new worldwide lifecycle gets fresh exposure, contribution, supplier, and
	# proof ledgers. Durable country episode receipts remain on their owners.
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_sources
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_targets
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_stage_entries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_receipt_entries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_episode_entries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_stage_entries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_values
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_contribution_receipts
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_countries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_receipts
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_severe_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_new_major_crisis_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_contribution_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.none }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.none }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_increase_total = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_event35_chaos_relief_total = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_stage
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_secured
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_reconstruction_complete
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_collapse
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_announcement_emitted
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_chaos_applied
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_international_recovery_chaos_receipt
	clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_super_event_emitted
	clear_global_event_target = great_depression_global_activation_source
}

great_depression_apply_supplier_collapse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_episode_id }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_applied
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_receipts value = great_depression_supplier_collapse_global_receipt_id }
		set_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_collapse
		set_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day = global.num_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
			subtract_from_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_stage
		clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_secured
		set_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_collapsed
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries value = THIS } }
			remove_from_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk_countries value = THIS }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = great_depression_event34_supplier_recipient_countries^num
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_event34_supplier_recipient_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
			}
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 0
				end = great_depression_event34_supplier_recipient_countries^num
				value = great_depression_supplier_collapse_index
				var:great_depression_event34_supplier_recipient_countries^great_depression_supplier_collapse_index = {
					set_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_dependent_shock
					if = {
						limit = { great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes }
						set_temp_variable = { great_depression_action_delta = constant:great_depression_action.strong_pressure }
						great_depression_apply_public_action_delta = yes
					}
					else_if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active }
						set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.severe }
						set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.dependent }
						set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day = global.num_days }
						add_to_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_conversion_delay_days }
						great_depression_apply_worldwide_pressure_modifier = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.supplier_collapse_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_global_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Derive a qualitative local world-pressure class from proved
# national and relationship conditions. The score remains hidden.
great_depression_classify_worldwide_pressure = {
	set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	if = {
		limit = { has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_idea = great_depression_scar_hollow_recovery
				has_country_flag = great_depression_recovery_hollow
			}
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects < constant:great_depression_scaling.large_economy_civilian_limit }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_faction_member = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_neighbor_country = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.one }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = great_depression_last_completed_episode_recovery_outcome
				value = constant:great_depression_recovery_outcome.strong
				compare = equals
			}
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score = constant:great_depression_api.two }
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score
		min = constant:great_depression_api.zero
		max = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_maximum
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.protected }
	set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.observed }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_severe_exposure_score compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.severe }
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.dependent }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day }
					NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals } }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day = global.num_days }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_conversion_delay_days }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_exposure_score value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_light_exposure_score compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.contraction }
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.pressured }
		clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day
	}
	else = { clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day }
}

great_depression_apply_worldwide_pressure_modifier = {
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage value = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.dependent compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage value = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.pressured compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
	}
	else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light } }
}

# COUNTRY scope. Register one bounded lightweight world-pressure row. The
# target validator owns all exclusions, so this helper cannot turn an active
# national crisis, boom supplier, special actor, or duplicate relationship
# into a second global penalty.
great_depression_register_worldwide_pressure_target = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			great_depression_is_valid_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries value = THIS } }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_country_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}
		}
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries value = THIS }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_episode_entries value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_stage_entries value = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		add_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
		great_depression_apply_worldwide_pressure_modifier = yes
		great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Evolution III performs this once at activation and then
# relies on the sparse daily adapter for later exact entries. The loop is
# deliberately capped by the global registry limit and never scans the world
# every day.
great_depression_initialize_worldwide_pressure_registry = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active }
		every_country = {
			set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.invalid }
			if = {
				limit = { great_depression_has_active_crisis = yes }
				set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.full_crisis }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = industrial_boom_active
						has_country_flag = industrial_boom_snapshot_frozen
					}
				}
				set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_classification = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.boom_supplier }
				great_depression_event34_supplier_adapter = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { great_depression_is_valid_worldwide_pressure_target = yes }
				great_depression_classify_worldwide_pressure = yes
				great_depression_register_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_register_late_worldwide_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			great_depression_is_valid_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
		}
		great_depression_classify_worldwide_pressure = yes
		great_depression_register_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. The first concrete global pressure application owns one
# announcement receipt and one guarded Chaos receipt. Evolution logging and
# global episode creation alone never emit the news or super-event.
great_depression_try_worldwide_announcement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_announcement_emitted }
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			check_variable = {
				var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
				value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_announcement_minimum
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_announcement_day = global.num_days }
		set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_announcement_emitted
		set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_super_event_emitted
		news_event = { id = chaosx.news.95 days = 2 }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_super_event_days = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_days }
		set_global_flag = { flag = super_event_visible days = great_depression_super_event_days value = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_super_event_slot }
		set_variable = { global.current_super_event_audio_id = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_super_event_audio_id }
		great_depression_play_worldwide_audio_for_player = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_announcement_emitted
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_chaos_applied }
		}
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_chaos_day = global.num_days }
		set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_chaos_applied
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_wave_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_global_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

great_depression_clear_global_pressure_registry = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			value = great_depression_global_pressure_clear_index
			var:global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^great_depression_global_pressure_clear_index = {
				clr_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
				remove_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
				clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_episode_id
				clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage
				clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day
				clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_classification
				great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
			}
		}
	}
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_episode_entries
	clear_array = global.great_depression_global_pressure_stage_entries
}

great_depression_update_global_pressure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active }
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				great_depression_has_active_crisis = yes
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
		remove_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
		clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_episode_id
		clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage
		clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_due_day
		clear_variable = great_depression_worldwide_classification
		great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			great_depression_is_valid_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
		}
		great_depression_classify_worldwide_pressure = yes
		great_depression_register_worldwide_pressure_target = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active }
			great_depression_apply_worldwide_pressure_modifier = yes
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_update_country_id = id }
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_found = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 0
				end = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
				value = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_index
				var:global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_index = {
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_country_id = id }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_country_id value = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_update_country_id compare = equals } }
						set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_index }
						set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_found = constant:great_depression_api.one }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row_found value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_stage_entries^great_depression_worldwide_pressure_row = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage }
			}
		}
		great_depression_try_worldwide_announcement = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY global-host scope. Registered world-pressure countries are updated
# once per Event 35 pulse interval; no recurring whole-world country scan is
# introduced after the one-time worldwide initialization.
great_depression_process_global_pressure_registry = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.pulse_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_pressure_pulse_day = global.num_days }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = 0
				end = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
				value = great_depression_global_pressure_index
				var:global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^great_depression_global_pressure_index = {
					great_depression_update_global_pressure = yes
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active }
						great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_event34_supplier_adapter = {
	# Event 34 keeps its supplier relationship here. Event 35 never mutates
	# Overheating directly and only consumes this owner-provided adapter flag.
	set_country_flag = great_depression_event34_supplier_pressure
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = industrial_boom_supplier_demand_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_supplier_episode_seen = industrial_boom_supplier_demand_receipt_id }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = industrial_boom_supplier_contract_at_risk
				has_country_flag = industrial_boom_supplier_contract
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
	}
	great_depression_register_supplier_risk = yes
}

great_depression_allocate_contagion_receipt = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_next_receipt_id } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_next_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.minimum_receipt_id }
	}
	set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_receipt_id = global.great_depression_next_receipt_id }
	add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_next_receipt_id = constant:great_depression_api.receipt_increment }
}

# COUNTRY target scope; ROOT is the active Event 35 source. Candidate scopes
# are relationship-bounded and the source registry hard cap is authoritative.
great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = {
	if = {
		limit = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
		save_event_target_as = great_depression_exposure_target
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
					has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
					NOT = { is_in_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets value = event_target:great_depression_exposure_target } }
					OR = {
						NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num }
						NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num value = constant:great_depression_event.contagion_source_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					}
				}
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = great_depression_candidate_exposure_stage }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_relationship_type = great_depression_candidate_relationship_type }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_relationship_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				great_depression_apply_exposure = yes
				clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_stage
				clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_relationship_type
				clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_relationship_proof
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY source scope. This one-time pass uses only engine-proved subject,
# faction, alliance, and land-neighbour scopes; duplicate links merge into the
# first, strongest row admitted by the capped source registry.
great_depression_initialize_contagion_network = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
		}
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_receipt_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_stage_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_relationship_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_peak_stage_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_material_source_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_aid_state_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_conversion_receipt_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_propagation_depth_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_target_cooldown_day_entries
		clear_array = great_depression_contagion_material_targets
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_contagion_propagation_depth } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_propagation_depth = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.pressured }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_relationship_type = constant:great_depression_exposure_relationship.subject_market }
		every_subject_country = {
			limit = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
			great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			overlord = { great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = yes }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_relationship_type = constant:great_depression_exposure_relationship.faction }
		every_faction_member = {
			limit = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
			great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.observed }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_relationship_type = constant:great_depression_exposure_relationship.allied }
		every_allied_country = {
			limit = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
			great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_candidate_relationship_type = constant:great_depression_exposure_relationship.neighbor }
		every_neighbor_country = {
			limit = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
			great_depression_register_contagion_candidate = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY source scope. The weekly national pulse walks only this source's
# capped rows. Contained or converted partners are skipped, and conversion is
# still guarded by the target-side delay and vulnerability contract.
great_depression_process_contagion_network = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
			has_variable = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num
			value = great_depression_contagion_source_index
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_relationship_type = great_depression_contagion_target_relationship_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.observed }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_material_source = constant:great_depression_contagion_material_source.relationship }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_aid_state = great_depression_contagion_target_aid_state_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_conversion_receipt = great_depression_contagion_target_conversion_receipt_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_propagation_depth = great_depression_contagion_target_propagation_depth_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_cooldown_day = great_depression_contagion_target_cooldown_day_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_exposure_relationship_type value = constant:great_depression_exposure_relationship.subject_market compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.pressured }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.deep_depression_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.pressured }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_material_source = constant:great_depression_contagion_material_source.severity }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.dependent }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_material_source = constant:great_depression_contagion_material_source.severity }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.paralysis_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_paralysis_severe_days value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.conversion_ready }
				set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_material_source = constant:great_depression_contagion_material_source.severity }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_target_stage_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index = great_depression_exposure_stage }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_exposure_stage value = great_depression_contagion_target_peak_stage_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_target_peak_stage_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index = great_depression_exposure_stage }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_target_material_source_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index = great_depression_exposure_material_source }
			set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_receipt_id = great_depression_contagion_target_receipt_entries^great_depression_contagion_source_index }
			set_variable = { great_depression_exposure_relationship_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
			var:great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^great_depression_contagion_source_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes
						NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_contained }
					}
					save_event_target_as = great_depression_exposure_target
					ROOT = { great_depression_apply_exposure = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_stage
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_relationship_type
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_relationship_proof
		clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_receipt_id
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_material_source
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_aid_state
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_conversion_receipt
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_propagation_depth
		clear_variable = great_depression_exposure_cooldown_day
	}
}

great_depression_clear_outgoing_contagion_network = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^num
			value = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_index
			var:great_depression_contagion_exposure_targets^great_depression_contagion_cleanup_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active }
						OR = {
							NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count }
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = less_than_or_equals }
						}
					}
					clr_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_exposure_open
					clr_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_contained
					clr_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_support_counted
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_financial_contagion }
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_countries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_episode_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_receipt_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_relationship_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_peak_stage_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_material_source_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_aid_state_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_conversion_receipt_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_propagation_depth_entries
					clear_array = great_depression_contagion_source_cooldown_day_entries
					clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_exposure_stage
					clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_source_episode_id
					clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_link_receipt_id
					clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_exposure_start_day
					clear_variable = great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_active }
						has_variable = great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_found = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
					for_loop_effect = {
						start = 0
						end = great_depression_contagion_source_countries^num
						value = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_index
						var:great_depression_contagion_source_countries^great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_index = {
							if = {
								limit = { tag = ROOT }
								set_temp_variable = { great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_index }
								set_temp_variable = { great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_found = constant:great_depression_api.one }
							}
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row_found value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals } }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_countries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_episode_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_receipt_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_relationship_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_peak_stage_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_material_source_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_aid_state_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_conversion_receipt_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_propagation_depth_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
						remove_from_array = { array = great_depression_contagion_source_cooldown_day_entries index = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_source_row }
					}
					set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_attributed_source_count = great_depression_contagion_source_countries^num }
					set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_exposure_stage = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.observed }
					for_loop_effect = {
						start = 0
						end = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries^num
						value = great_depression_contagion_cleanup_remaining_stage_index
						if = {
							limit = {
								check_variable = {
									var = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries^great_depression_contagion_cleanup_remaining_stage_index
									value = great_depression_contagion_exposure_stage
									compare = greater_than
								}
							}
							set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_exposure_stage = great_depression_contagion_source_stage_entries^great_depression_contagion_cleanup_remaining_stage_index }
						}
					}
					set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_source_episode_id = great_depression_contagion_source_episode_entries^0 }
					set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_link_receipt_id = great_depression_contagion_source_receipt_entries^0 }
					great_depression_refresh_costs = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_apply_exposure = {
	# Source country inputs: great_depression_exposure_target, exposure stage,
	# source episode proof, and a positive relationship proof.
	if = {
		limit = {
			great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_financial_contagion
			has_event_target = great_depression_exposure_target
			event_target:great_depression_exposure_target = { great_depression_is_valid_contagion_network_target = yes }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_exposure_stage value = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.observed compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_exposure_stage value = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.resolved compare = less_than_or_equals }
