										set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_last_attributable_conversion_receipt = PREV.great_depression_contagion_conversion_receipt }
										if = {
											limit = { has_variable = great_depression_contagion_conversion_count }
											add_to_variable = { great_depression_contagion_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
										}
										else = { set_variable = { great_depression_contagion_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
									}
								}
							}
						}
						set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.contagion_conversion_chaos }
						great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = yes
					}
					clear_array = great_depression_pending_material_contagion_sources
					clear_array = great_depression_pending_material_contagion_episode_entries
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_activate_social_collapse = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.systemic_breakdown_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_social_extreme_days value = constant:great_depression_event.social_extreme_gate_days compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_stability < constant:great_depression_thresholds.social_peace_stability_floor
			NOT = { has_civil_war = yes }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_event6_front_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_eligible }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
		}
		great_depression_select_social_actor = yes
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
		set_country_flag = great_depression_organized_strike
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_organized_actor_proof
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.organized_protest }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.organized_strike }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_incident_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_ai = no
				has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_started_at_opening
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_tracking_invalidated }
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_achievement_social_peace_candidate
		}
	}
}

great_depression_resolve_social_collapse = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_organized_strike
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_sector_conflict
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_national_unrest
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_government_crisis
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_revolutionary_breakdown
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_active
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.resolved }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.resolved }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_resolution_day = global.num_days }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_successful_action_count }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_successful_action_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
		else = { set_variable = { great_depression_successful_action_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Selects one qualitative movement family from proved local
# conditions. The selector is deterministic so a save cannot reroll the
# actor merely because the weekly pulse reached a different branch.
great_depression_select_social_actor = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_social_actor_family }
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_actor_family
					value = constant:great_depression_api.zero
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.trade_union_coalition }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
					}
				}
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.deep_depression_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.syndicalist_occupation }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center
					NOT = { is_capital = yes }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.regional_autonomy }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = great_depression_contagion_material_observed
					has_country_flag = great_depression_supplier_contract_at_risk
					has_country_flag = great_depression_event34_supplier_pressure
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.business_creditor }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = communism }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.communist_council }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = fascism }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.nationalist_mass }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_at_war = yes }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.military_emergency }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_government = neutrality }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.traditional_authority }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = great_depression_humanitarian_pressure_observed
					has_country_flag = great_depression_migration_pressure_observed
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_family = constant:great_depression_social_actor.rural_protest }
		}
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_actor_proof
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_actor_selection_day = global.num_days }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_actor_family
					value = constant:great_depression_social_actor.regional_autonomy
					compare = equals
				}
				any_controlled_state = {
					has_state_flag = great_depression_center
					NOT = { is_capital = yes }
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_regional_movement_proof
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				num_of_controlled_states > constant:random_civil_war_value.one
				num_divisions > constant:random_civil_war_value.one
				has_manpower > constant:great_depression_api.zero
				num_of_military_factories > constant:great_depression_api.zero
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_force_package_proof
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Factory occupation is an Event 35 social status, not a
# military-control shortcut. It is assigned once to the first eligible center
# in the deterministic center registry and remains episode-bound.
great_depression_register_factory_occupation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_social_sector_conflict
			check_variable = { var = great_depression_social_actor_family value = constant:great_depression_social_actor.syndicalist_occupation compare = equals }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_factory_occupation_active }
			has_variable = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_factory_occupation_found = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = great_depression_center_state_ids^num
			value = great_depression_factory_occupation_index
			var:great_depression_center_state_ids^great_depression_factory_occupation_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = great_depression_factory_occupation_found value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = equals }
						is_owned_by = ROOT
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
						has_state_flag = great_depression_center
						OR = {
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_idled
							has_state_flag = great_depression_center_shuttered
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = great_depression_center_factory_occupation_active
					set_variable = { great_depression_center_factory_occupation_episode_id = ROOT.great_depression_active_episode_id }
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_factory_occupation_state_id_request = id }
					ROOT = {
						set_country_flag = great_depression_factory_occupation_active
						set_variable = { great_depression_factory_occupation_state_id = great_depression_factory_occupation_state_id_request }
					}
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_factory_occupation_found = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. A social incident reaches the Chaos layer only after the
# ladder proves national unrest. The receipt makes the consequence idempotent
# while keeping the public Severity as the only persistent player-facing
# number.
great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_outcome_chaos_receipt }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_successful
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_regional_autonomy_rejected
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_civil_conflict_started
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
				has_country_flag = great_depression_social_response_failed
			}
		}
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_outcome_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_outcome_episode_id = great_depression_active_episode_id }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_outcome_chaos_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.social_outcome_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

great_depression_apply_civil_conflict_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_chaos_receipt } }
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_civil_conflict_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_chaos_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.civil_conflict_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Advances one social ladder rung per pulse. Each rung has an
# explicit receipt so a long-running incident cannot jump from a grievance to
# revolutionary breakdown without the intervening national proof.
great_depression_progress_social_ladder = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_social_ladder_day } }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_day = global.num_days }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed = great_depression_social_ladder_day }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_social_ladder_stage value = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.sector_conflict compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_social_actor_family value = constant:great_depression_social_actor.syndicalist_occupation compare = equals }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_factory_occupation_active }
			}
			great_depression_register_factory_occupation = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_stage
					value = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.organized_protest
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed
					value = constant:great_depression_social_timing.sector_conflict_days
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.sector_conflict }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_sector_conflict
			great_depression_register_factory_occupation = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_stage
					value = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.sector_conflict
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed
					value = constant:great_depression_social_timing.national_unrest_days
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.national_unrest }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_national_unrest
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_major_incident_proof
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_stage
					value = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.national_unrest
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed
					value = constant:great_depression_social_timing.government_crisis_days
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.government_crisis }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_government_crisis
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.emergency_government }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_stage
					value = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.government_crisis
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_social_ladder_elapsed
					value = constant:great_depression_social_timing.revolutionary_breakdown_days
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_ladder_stage = constant:great_depression_social_ladder.revolutionary_breakdown }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_revolutionary_breakdown
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.civil_conflict_eligible }
		}
		great_depression_queue_social_extreme_outcome = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. Resolves the highest social rung only through the dedicated
# outcome gate. Coup and regional crisis receive player-facing event choices;
# civil conflict is handed to Event 021, and every failed route falls back to
# a recorded non-war breakdown instead of inventing a faction.
great_depression_queue_social_extreme_outcome = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_social_incident_open
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued }
			great_depression_social_ladder_is_at_least_government_crisis = yes
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_review_day }
				check_variable = { var = global.num_days value = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_review_day compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		great_depression_incident_select_social_extreme_outcome = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.coup_attempt compare = equals }
				great_depression_social_coup_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_attempted
			set_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.coup_crisis }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_day = global.num_days }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr35.4 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.regional_uprising compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.separatist_conflict compare = equals }
				}
				great_depression_social_separatist_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_separatist_attempted
			set_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_regional_autonomy_fallback
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.separatist_crisis }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_day = global.num_days }
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr35.5 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.full_civil_war compare = equals }
				great_depression_social_civil_conflict_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_civil_conflict_attempted
			set_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_eligible
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.civil_conflict_eligible }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_day = global.num_days }
			great_depression_start_shared_civil_conflict = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
				set_country_flag = great_depression_social_civil_conflict_started
				great_depression_apply_civil_conflict_consequence = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
				great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.negotiated_power_transfer compare = equals }
				great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_government_settled
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_negotiated_transfer_observed
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.political_mandate }
			add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_resolution_stability_relief
			great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.emergency_dictatorship compare = equals }
				great_depression_social_coup_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
			set_country_flag = great_depression_emergency_government_formed
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_active
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_dictatorship_observed
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.emergency_government }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_start_day = global.num_days }
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_due_day = global.num_days }
			add_to_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_due_day = constant:great_depression_social_timing.emergency_mandate_days }
			set_politics = { elections_allowed = no }
			great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = great_depression_incident_kind value = constant:great_depression_incident_kind.revolutionary_coalition compare = equals }
				great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_gate = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_revolutionary_coalition_observed
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.political_mandate }
			great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_gate = yes }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_extreme_outcome_queued
			set_country_flag = great_depression_social_nonwar_breakdown
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.political_mandate }
			add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_coup_stability_delta
			great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
		}
	}
}

great_depression_execute_social_coup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis
			has_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_attempted
			NOT = { has_civil_war = yes }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_government = democratic } set_politics = { ruling_party = democratic elections_allowed = no } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_government = communism } set_politics = { ruling_party = communism elections_allowed = no } }
		else_if = { limit = { has_government = fascism } set_politics = { ruling_party = fascism elections_allowed = no } }
		else = { set_politics = { ruling_party = neutrality elections_allowed = no } }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_successful
		set_country_flag = great_depression_emergency_government_formed
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_active
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.emergency_government }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_start_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_due_day = global.num_days }
		add_to_variable = { great_depression_social_emergency_mandate_due_day = constant:great_depression_social_timing.emergency_mandate_days }
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_coup_stability_delta
		great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
	}
}

great_depression_stand_down_social_coup = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_coup_crisis
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_coup_stood_down
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_resolution_stability_relief
		great_depression_resolve_social_collapse = yes
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_collapse_resolved
	}
}

great_depression_recognize_regional_autonomy = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_regional_autonomy_recognized
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_resolution_stability_relief
		great_depression_resolve_social_collapse = yes
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_collapse_resolved
	}
}

great_depression_integrate_regional_autonomy = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis }
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_separatist_crisis
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_regional_autonomy_rejected
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_regional_stability_delta
		great_depression_apply_social_outcome_consequence = yes
		great_depression_resolve_social_collapse = yes
		set_country_flag = great_depression_social_collapse_resolved
	}
}

great_depression_apply_national_breakdown_consequence = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chaos_receipt } }
		great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
		set_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chaos_receipt
		set_temp_variable = { chaos_change = constant:great_depression_event.social_outcome_chaos }
		great_depression_apply_local_chaos_receipt = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. These three outcomes are the guarded baseline National
# Breakdown resolution surface. The civil-conflict route is never exposed
# unless the complete baseline force-package gate succeeds; every failed
# shared handoff resolves through the same idempotent non-war receipt.
great_depression_resolve_national_breakdown_emergency_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved }
		}
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_open
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_nonwar_outcome
		set_country_flag = great_depression_emergency_government_formed
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.emergency_government }
		set_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_day = global.num_days }
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.national_breakdown_stability_delta
		great_depression_apply_national_breakdown_consequence = yes
	}
}

great_depression_resolve_national_breakdown_cabinet_replacement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved }
		}
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_open
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_nonwar_outcome
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_cabinet_restored
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_legitimacy_repaired
		set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.political_mandate }
		set_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_day = global.num_days }
		add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.social_resolution_stability_relief
		great_depression_apply_national_breakdown_consequence = yes
	}
}

great_depression_resolve_national_breakdown_civil_conflict = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved }
			great_depression_baseline_breakdown_civil_conflict_gate = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
		clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_open
		set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved
		set_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_eligible
		set_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_day = global.num_days }
		great_depression_start_shared_civil_conflict = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = random_civil_war_active }
			set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_civil_conflict_started
			great_depression_apply_civil_conflict_consequence = yes
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_civil_conflict_eligible
			set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_nonwar_outcome
			set_country_flag = great_depression_emergency_government_formed
			set_variable = { great_depression_social_stage = constant:great_depression_social_stage.emergency_government }
			add_stability = constant:great_depression_action.national_breakdown_stability_delta
			great_depression_apply_national_breakdown_consequence = yes
		}
	}
}

great_depression_process_national_breakdown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_open
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_social_collapse }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolved }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued }
			has_variable = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_day
			NOT = { has_active_mission = great_depression_prevent_national_breakdown }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_national_breakdown_elapsed = great_depression_national_breakdown_chain_day }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = great_depression_national_breakdown_elapsed
					value = constant:great_depression_event.breakdown_mission_days
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_warning
			set_country_flag = great_depression_national_breakdown_resolution_queued
			country_event = { id = chaosx.nr35.6 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_try_worldwide_conversion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination compare = equals }
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_exposure_targets^num }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = global.great_depression_global_exposure_targets^num
						value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_country_cap
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day } }
			great_depression_run_worldwide_conversion = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_conversion_elapsed = global.num_days }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_conversion_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_conversion_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_conversion_interval_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				great_depression_run_worldwide_conversion = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

great_depression_run_worldwide_conversion = {
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day = global.num_days }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = great_depression_global_activation_source
			event_target:great_depression_global_activation_source = { exists = yes }
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		event_target:great_depression_global_activation_source = {
			save_event_target_as = great_depression_call_worldwide_origin
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor }
					check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor value = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_index = global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor }
		add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_cursor = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		var:global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^great_depression_worldwide_conversion_index = {
			if = {
				limit = { great_depression_is_valid_worldwide_target = yes }
				great_depression_allocate_receipt = yes
				set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_receipt_id = great_depression_last_receipt_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_contract_supplied = constant:great_depression_api.contract_version }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_caller_type = constant:great_depression_caller.worldwide_depression_conversion }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_event_id = constant:great_depression_api.event_35_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_source_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_receipt_id = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_receipt_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_pacing_mode = constant:great_depression_pacing.conversion_no_pacing }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_history_mode = constant:great_depression_history_mode.worldwide }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_report_policy = constant:great_depression_report_policy.queue }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_news_policy = constant:great_depression_news_policy.queue }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_floor = constant:great_depression_evolution.second_great_depression }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_evolution_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_input_mode = constant:great_depression_severity_input.severity_profile }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_starting_severity_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_deepen_amount_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile = constant:great_depression_severity_profile.worldwide_conversion }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_severity_profile_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_opening_shock_profile = constant:great_depression_shock_profile.worldwide_conversion }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_episode_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_pressure_stage = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_stage }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_conversion_receipt_id = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_receipt_id }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_conversion_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				set_variable = { great_depression_call_worldwide_origin_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
				great_depression_start_or_deepen = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_started value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_call_deepened value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
						}
					}
					add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_sources value = event_target:great_depression_global_activation_source }
					add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_targets value = THIS }
					add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_stage_entries value = constant:great_depression_exposure_stage.dependent }
					add_to_array = { array = global.great_depression_global_exposure_receipt_entries value = great_depression_worldwide_conversion_receipt_id }
					set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_conversion_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
					set_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_target
					clr_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_light }
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction }
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = great_depression_global_contraction_severe }
					remove_ideas = great_depression_global_contraction
					if = {
						limit = { has_variable = global.great_depression_global_conversion_count }
						add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
					}
					else = { set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.one } }
					event_target:great_depression_global_activation_source = {
						set_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_source
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# GLOBAL lifecycle aggregate. It walks only the one-shot worldwide registry,
# weights each surviving civilian economy by its real factory base, and marks
# pressure ready only when severe economies are no more than the tuned share.
# The episode cutoff distinguishes a new major crisis during final proof from
# national depressions that were already part of the worldwide shock.
great_depression_recalculate_global_recovery_pressure = {
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_severe_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_new_major_crisis_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than }
		}
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^num
			value = great_depression_global_industrial_pressure_index
			var:global.great_depression_global_pressure_countries^great_depression_global_industrial_pressure_index = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = yes
						great_depression_has_viable_economy = yes
					}
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight = num_of_civilian_factories }
					multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight = constant:great_depression_api.two }
					add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight = num_of_military_factories }
					add_to_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight = num_of_naval_factories }
					clamp_temp_variable = {
						var = great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight
						min = constant:great_depression_api.one
						max = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_maximum
					}
					add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight = great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight }
					if = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								AND = {
									great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
									check_variable = { var = great_depression_depression_severity value = constant:great_depression_event.deep_depression_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals }
								}
								AND = {
									has_country_flag = great_depression_worldwide_pressure_active
									check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_classification value = constant:great_depression_worldwide_classification.severe compare = greater_than_or_equals }
								}
							}
						}
						add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_severe_industrial_weight = great_depression_global_country_industrial_weight }
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_major = yes
							great_depression_active_crisis_is_coherent = yes
							check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than_or_equals }
							check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff compare = greater_than_or_equals }
						}
						add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_global_new_major_crisis_count = constant:great_depression_api.one }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_severe_share_left = global.great_depression_global_severe_industrial_weight }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_severe_share_left = constant:great_depression_event.public_value_maximum }
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_severe_share_right = global.great_depression_global_registered_industrial_weight }
		multiply_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_severe_share_right = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_severe_industrial_share_percent }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_severe_share_left value = great_depression_global_severe_share_right compare = less_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready = constant:great_depression_api.one }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_new_major_crisis_count value = constant:great_depression_api.zero compare = greater_than } }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = less_than }
		}
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_process_global_episode = {
	great_depression_register_supplier_risk = yes
	great_depression_process_global_pressure_registry = yes
	great_depression_recalculate_global_recovery_pressure = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved
			has_variable = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_supplier_collapse_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_supplier_collapse_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_supplier_collapse_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			clr_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_supplier_risk_final_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.shock compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_stage_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_stage_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.contraction }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.contraction }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.contraction compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_stage_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_stage_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_pressure_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.coordination compare = equals }
		}
		great_depression_try_worldwide_conversion = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_supplier_risk_final_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_contribution_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_contribution_industrial_weight value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_industrial_weight_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.num_days }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_stage_day }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_stage_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_coordination_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = less_than } }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = global.num_days }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = global.great_depression_next_episode_id }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_stage_day }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_supplier_collapse_unresolved }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_supplier_risk_final_cap compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				has_variable = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_contribution_countries^num value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_contribution_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_contribution_industrial_weight value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_industrial_weight_minimum compare = greater_than_or_equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_pressure_ready value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = equals }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_stage_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_coordination_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof value = constant:great_depression_api.one compare = less_than } }
					set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.one }
					set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = global.num_days }
					set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_episode_cutoff = global.great_depression_next_episode_id }
					set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_supplier_collapse = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
				}
				else = {
					set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_proof_elapsed = global.num_days }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_proof_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day }
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_proof_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_thresholds.global_recovery_proof_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.international_reconstruction }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
						set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.international_reconstruction }
						set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_stage
						set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_secured
						great_depression_apply_international_recovery_chaos_relief = yes
						great_depression_evaluate_global_reconstruction_contributors = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.international_reconstruction compare = equals }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.num_days }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { great_depression_global_stage_elapsed = global.great_depression_global_stage_day }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_global_stage_elapsed value = constant:great_depression_event.global_retirement_days compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.retired }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
			set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_reconstruction_complete
			great_depression_clear_global_pressure_registry = yes
			clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			clear_global_event_target = great_depression_global_activation_source
		}
	}
}

great_depression_activate_worldwide_episode = {
	great_depression_initialize_global_runtime = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_evolution_second_great_depression
			NOT = { has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active }
		}
		great_depression_clear_global_pressure_registry = yes
		great_depression_reset_global_episode_ledgers = yes
		set_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_episode_id = global.great_depression_next_episode_id }
		add_to_variable = { global.great_depression_next_episode_id = constant:great_depression_api.episode_increment }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.shock }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_highest_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.shock }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event = constant:great_depression_api.event_35_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.deepening }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_active_source_event_id }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_event = great_depression_active_source_event_id }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_active_source_episode_id }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_episode = great_depression_active_source_episode_id }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = great_depression_active_source_caller }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_source_caller value = constant:great_depression_caller.industrial_boom_collapse compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.industrial_boom }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_source_caller value = constant:great_depression_caller.financial_contagion_conversion compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.contagion }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_active_source_caller value = constant:great_depression_caller.worldwide_depression_conversion compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_activation_source_type = constant:great_depression_source.worldwide }
			}
		}
		save_global_event_target_as = great_depression_global_activation_source
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_risk = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_supplier_collapse_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_last_conversion_day = global.num_days }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_conversion_count = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		great_depression_initialize_worldwide_pressure_registry = yes
		great_depression_try_worldwide_announcement = yes
		great_depression_register_supplier_risk = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY scope. A new major-country crisis during Fragmented Recovery or
# International Reconstruction invalidates the current global proof period.
# The historical supplier-collapse receipt remains intact, but the active
# proof must earn itself again under the same stable-condition checks.
great_depression_reset_global_recovery_proof = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = great_depression_active
			is_major = yes
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.international_reconstruction compare = equals }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_recovery_proof_day = constant:great_depression_api.zero }
		clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_stage
		clr_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_recovery_secured
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.great_depression_global_stage value = constant:great_depression_global_stage.international_reconstruction compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage = constant:great_depression_global_stage.recovery }
			set_variable = { global.great_depression_global_stage_day = global.num_days }
		}
	}
}

great_depression_play_worldwide_audio_for_player = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = great_depression_worldwide_episode_active
			is_ai = no
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = great_depression_worldwide_audio_episode_id }
				NOT = { check_variable = { var = great_depression_worldwide_audio_episode_id value = global.great_depression_global_episode_id compare = equals } }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { global.current_super_event_audio_id = constant:great_depression_event.worldwide_super_event_audio_id }
		set_variable = { great_depression_worldwide_audio_episode_id = global.great_depression_global_episode_id }
		play_current_super_event_audio = yes
	}
}
